### [153] [RubricRM: Generative Reward Modeling via Dynamic Rubrics for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26956)
*Zijian Kan,Wei Wang,Long Luo,Bing Zhao,Xuan Ren,Weixu Qiao,Wenbo Li,Hu Wei,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricRM，一种成对生成式奖励建模框架，先为每个输入生成包含评估维度、权重和评分标准的具体rubric，再据此对候选图像打分，用于文本到图像生成和指令式图像编辑的奖励模型，在多个基准上优于现有专用奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉奖励模型大多使用单一标量分数或依赖固定评估标准，无法适应不同指令，导致可解释性和任务敏感性受限。文本到图像生成和指令式图像编辑中不同输入需要不同评估维度，需要更自适应的奖励模型。

Method: 提出RubricRM成对生成式奖励建模框架，先为每个输入生成特定rubric（含评估维度、权重、评分标准），再按rubric给候选图像打分。采用两阶段训练：监督微调教模型rubric评分范式，GRPO通过细粒度维度级奖励进一步优化评分。

Result: 在多个文本到图像生成和图像编辑基准上，RubricRM优于现有专用奖励模型，并使用更小的骨干网络与强专有MLLM评判者保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RubricRM通过输入自适应的rubric生成与维度级奖励训练，提升了奖励模型的可解释性和任务敏感性，为视觉生成对齐提供更优的奖励信号。

Abstract: Reward models play an essential role in aligning visual generative models, yet most existing visual reward models use a single scalar score or rely on fixed criteria that cannot adapt to different instructions. This limits both interpretability and task sensitivity, especially for text-to-image generation and instruction-based image editing, where different inputs require different evaluation dimensions. We propose RubricRM, a pairwise generative reward modeling framework that first produces an input-specific rubric with evaluation dimensions, weights, and scoring criteria, and then applies the rubric to score candidate images. We train dedicated RubricRM models for text-to-image generation and image editing using a two-stage training pipeline: supervised fine-tuning teaches the model the rubric-based scoring paradigm, while GRPO further improves scoring through fine-grained dimension-level rewards. Experiments on multiple generation and editing benchmarks show that RubricRM outperforms existing specialized reward models and remains competitive with strong proprietary MLLM judges despite using smaller backbones. Our models, data, and code are available at https://github.com/zijiankan/RubricRM.

</details>


### [154] [Geo-LoRA: Geometry-Aware Subspace Evolution for Low-Rank Adaptation in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26960)
*Yibo Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Geo-LoRA是一个几何感知的框架，通过显式调控LoRA低秩子空间在连续学习中的演化，解决无重播类增量学习（CIL）中子空间更新缺乏几何控制的问题，在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无重播CIL中的LoRA适配器更新任务时，低秩子空间缺乏几何约束，导致共享表示不稳定，且任务专属更新反复坍缩到先前占据的方向，影响增量学习的稳定性和可塑性平衡。

Method: Geo-LoRA包含三条核心机制：(1) 共享分支的SPP（子空间投影保持）约束连续更新在Grassmann流形上平滑演化；(2) ACSA（自适应核心-松弛对齐）将子空间转变分解为主成分和残差成分，对齐主成分并调制残差以平衡稳定性和可塑性；(3) 任务专属分支的MCBO（中位数校准块重叠）通过归一化投影重叠施加统计约束，惩罚过度的子空间重用以减少子空间拥挤。所有约束无需引入超出标准LoRA的新适配器类型。

Result: Geo-LoRA在多个基准数据集和不同任务长度上一致达到state-of-the-art性能，验证了几何约束对低秩子空间持续演化的有效性。

Conclusion: Geo-LoRA为持续低秩适配提供了规范的几何形式化框架，通过联合调控跨层、跨任务的LoRA子空间演化，在无需重播数据的前提下有效解决了类增量学习中的子空间不稳定与拥挤问题。

Abstract: Rehearsal-free class-incremental learning (CIL) with LoRA adapters remains challenging because the low-rank subspaces updated across tasks evolve without geometric control, causing unstable shared representations and repetitive collapse of task-specific updates into previously occupied directions. We introduce Geo-LoRA, a geometry-aware framework that explicitly regulates how low-rank subspaces, both shared and task-specific, evolve during continual learning. For the shared branch, Subspace Projection Preservation (SPP) constrains consecutive updates to follow smooth trajectories on the Grassmann manifold, and Adaptive Core-Slack Alignment (ACSA) decomposes transitions into principal and residual components, aligning the former while modulating the latter to balance stability and plasticity. For the task-specific branch, Median-Calibrated Block Overlap (MCBO) imposes a statistical constraint via normalized projection overlap, penalizing excessive reuse to mitigate subspace crowding. These constraints jointly regulate the evolution of all LoRA subspaces across layers and tasks without introducing additional adapter types beyond standard LoRA. Geo-LoRA provides a principled geometric formulation for continual low-rank adaptation and consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmark datasets and different task lengths.

</details>


### [155] [TempJail: Temporal Jailbreak Attacks against Image-to-Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26971)
*Qi Lu,Zehui Guo,David Yuanda Gan,Zijing Li,Hengda Zhang,Weijun Xu,Qiankun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TempJail，一个针对图像到视频（I2V）生成模型的时序越狱攻击框架。研究发现I2V系统存在时序漏洞：不安全语义可通过时间维度的语义组合逐步涌现，而非仅存在于单帧。TempJail通过将恶意提示分解为初始帧视觉条件和时序文本指令实现时序抽象，并通过图像侧的潜在扰动注入和文本侧的无害化改写实现语义伪装，在Kling、Seedance、Veo和PixVerse等商业模型上分别提升攻击成功率23.3%（GPT-5.2评估）和22.0%（人工评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对视频生成模型的安全研究主要关注单帧违规的越狱攻击，忽略了视频生成模型所独有的时间维度。作者发现I2V系统中存在一种时序漏洞：不安全语义并非在单帧中出现，而是通过时间上的语义组合逐步产生。此外，此类攻击面临两个关键挑战：时序抽象（如何将恶意语义分解到时间维度）和语义伪装（如何使恶意语义在空间和时间上均绕过安全过滤器）。

Method: TempJail框架包含两个核心模块：1）时序抽象：将目标恶意描述分解为初始帧的视觉条件和时序文本指令；2）语义伪装：在图像侧，将语义注入建模为扩散采样中的受控潜在扰动，并引入预训练编码器的梯度引导；在文本侧，将提示词改写为无害的"主体-动作-场景"模板，既能绕过安全过滤器又能保留时序引导。在黑盒推理阶段，两种模态协同作用，使恶意语义随时间逐步触发。

Result: 在Kling、Seedance、Veo和PixVerse等闭源商业模型上进行实验，TempJail相比此前最先进方法，在GPT-5.2评估下攻击成功率提升23.3%，在人工评估下提升22.0%。

Conclusion: TempJail揭示了I2V生成模型中存在的时间维度安全漏洞，证明了通过跨时间语义组合实现越狱攻击的可行性，为该领域的安全防护研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In recent years, image-to-video (I2V) generation models have made remarkable progress in subject consistency and temporal coherence, enabling high quality video synthesis. However, these advances also introduce new safety risks. Existing studies mainly focus on jailbreak attacks involving single frame violations, while largely overlooking the temporal dimension unique to video generation models. In this paper, we investigate three attack scenarios and uncover a temporal vulnerability in I2V systems: unsafe semantics may emerge not from a single frame, but from semantic composition over time. We further identify two key challenges in such attacks: temporal abstraction and semantic camouflage. To address these issues, we propose TempJail, a novel temporal jailbreak framework for I2V systems. For temporal abstraction, we decompose a target malicious caption into an initial frame visual condition and a temporal text instruction. For semantic camouflage, on the image side we model semantic injection as controlled latent perturbation in diffusion sampling and introduce gradient guidance from pretrained encoders. On the text side, we rewrite the caption into an innocuous ``subject-action-scene'' template that bypasses safety filters while preserving temporal guidance. In the black-box inference phase, these two modalities jointly enable malicious semantics to be gradually triggered over time. Experiments on closed-source commercial models, including Kling, Seedance, Veo and PixVerse, show that TempJail improves attack success rate over prior state-of-the-art methods by 23.3\% under GPT-5.2 evaluation and 22.0\% under human evaluation. Our codes are available at \href{https://github.com/luqi-glory/TempJail}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [156] [Aphanta: Diagnosing Task-Aligned Image-Edited Intermediates for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26993)
*Hengyuan Xu,Wei Cheng,Yumeng Ji,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Gang Yu,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aphanta，一个自动化任务发现与闭环诊断框架，用于评估"多模态大语言模型(MLLM)→图像编辑器→MLLM"流水线的有效性。通过对比直接推理、编辑器生成的中间结果和理想化参考中间结果三种条件，分离了潜在的视觉提升空间与当前编辑器的实际效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式视觉中间表示可以外化空间证据和更新后的视觉状态，帮助多模态大语言模型进行推理，但其实际效用高度依赖于图像编辑器能否忠实实现所需的变换。然而目前缺乏系统性的评估框架来区分：模型本身潜在的视觉能力提升空间 与 当前编辑器工具的实际可靠性。

Method: Aphanta框架设计了三种评估条件：(1) 直接推理（不经过编辑器）；(2) 使用编辑器生成的中间结果进行推理；(3) 使用理想化参考中间结果进行推理。通过对比这三种条件的表现，可以量化归纳出视觉头(visual headroom)与编辑器实际效用之间的差距。研究覆盖20个候选任务、多个编辑器-MLLM组合，并在正向任务子集上构建了整合的Qwen流水线进行验证。

Result: 编辑器的效用高度依赖任务类型。增益集中在视觉线索注入、 grounding 和反事实状态实现等任务上；而需要符号敏感构建或结构外推的中间表示则可靠性显著降低。在选定的正向任务子集上，整合的Qwen流水线将平均任务得分从0.343提升至0.445（+10.2分，相对提升29.7%）。完整研究保留了过滤后和失败的任务以揭示能力边界。

Conclusion: 图像编辑应被视为专门的视觉工作区而非通用的推理机制。Aphanta可作为一个可复用的协议，用于测量任务-表示对齐、编辑器实现能力和下游流水线效用。

Abstract: Explicit visual intermediates can help multimodal large language models (MLLMs) externalize spatial evidence and updated visual states, but their utility depends on whether an image editor can faithfully realize the required transformation. We introduce \textbf{Aphanta}, an automated task-discovery and closed-loop diagnostic framework for the MLLM -> image editor -> MLLM pipeline. Aphanta evaluates three conditions---direct reasoning, reasoning with an editor-generated intermediate, and reasoning with an idealized reference intermediate---to separate potential visual headroom from the practical utility of current editors. Across 20 candidate tasks and multiple editor--MLLM combinations, we find that utility is strongly task-conditioned. Gains concentrate in visual cue injection, grounding, and counterfactual state realization, whereas intermediates requiring symbol-sensitive construction or structural extrapolation are substantially less reliable. On the selected positive-task subset, our consolidated Qwen pipeline improves the mean task score from 0.343 to 0.445 ($+10.2$ points; $+29.7\%$ relative), while the full study also retains filtered and unsuccessful tasks to expose the boundary. These results position image editing as a specialized visual workspace rather than a universal reasoning mechanism, and establish Aphanta as a reusable protocol for measuring task--representation alignment, editor realization, and downstream pipeline utility.

</details>


### [157] [Virtual iEEG from Scalp EEG: Charting the Landscape of Source Imaging, Intracranial Inference and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26998)
*Dongyi He,Xiangkai Wang,Hongjie Yan,Luping Song,Wai Ting Siok,Nizhuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"虚拟颅内脑电（virtual iEEG）"的综述性论文，系统梳理了从头皮脑电推断颅内脑电信号的研究框架，提出了以靶标为中心的分类体系（事件推断、特征转换、波形重建），并从可预测性、可观测性、可识别性、保真度和实用性五个维度评估了现有证据。结论是当前研究可以支持部分颅内事件、低频成分和任务相关表征的推断，但无法唯一恢复任意的接触级脑电活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内脑电（iEEG）具有优异的时间精度和空间特异性，但由于其侵入性和受限的解剖覆盖范围而难以常规使用。这种限制促使研究者探索从头皮脑电到颅内脑电的推断方法，即"虚拟iEEG"。然而该领域缺乏统一的框架和评价标准，存在概念混淆和验证方法不严谨的问题，因此需要一篇综述来系统梳理研究现状、明确术语边界，并提出严格的验证标准。

Method: 本文采用回顾性综述方法，提出一个以靶标为中心（target-centred）的分析框架，将虚拟iEEG任务划分为三类：事件推断（event inference）、特征翻译（feature translation）和波形重建（waveform reconstruction）。同时区分了可预测性（predictability）、可观测性（observability）、可识别性（identifiability）、保真度（fidelity）和实用性（utility）五个概念维度。证据评估标准包括：队列独立性、解剖与频谱覆盖范围、训练-测试分离以及靶病人适应性。

Result: 综述发现：现有研究能够支持对特定颅内事件、低频成分和任务相关神经表征的推断，但尚不能实现对任意接触级脑电活动的唯一恢复。作者进一步指出，更强有力的验证需要适当的对照实验、源成像基线比较、不确定性评估和增量实用性测试。未来进展依赖独立的配对数据集，以及通过前瞻性证据证明虚拟iEEG相对头皮脑电和脑电源成像的额外价值。

Conclusion: 虚拟iEEG在部分任务场景中展现出可行性，但距离成为可靠的替代工具仍有显著差距。该领域的科学严谨性需要大幅提升，包括建立统一的概念框架、严格的验证设计以及独立的评估数据集。只有通过严格的增量价值证明，虚拟iEEG才能确立其在临床和科研中的实际地位。

Abstract: Intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) provides temporally precise and spatially specific access to neural activity from focal and deep brain regions, but its invasiveness and restricted anatomical coverage limit routine use. These constraints have motivated scalp-to-intracranial inference, termed virtual iEEG when model outputs carry iEEG-defined event, feature, representation, or contact-level waveform semantics. This review presents a target-centred framework distinguishing event inference, feature translation, and waveform reconstruction, while separating predictability from observability, identifiability, fidelity, and utility. Evidence is evaluated according to cohort independence, anatomical and spectral coverage, train--test separation, and target-patient adaptation. Current studies support inference of selected intracranial events, low-frequency components, and task-related representations, but not unique recovery of arbitrary contact-level activity. Stronger validation requires appropriate controls, source-imaging baselines, uncertainty assessment, and incremental-utility testing. Future progress depends on independent paired datasets and prospective evidence that virtual iEEG adds value beyond scalp EEG and EEG source imaging.

</details>


### [158] [MVC-Bench: Benchmarking Calibration of Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27004)
*Ashshak Sharifdeen,Shihab Aaqil Ahamed,Ufaq Khan,Muhammad Akhtar Munir Sujair Ibrahim,Mohamed Rafeek Mareer Ahamed,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVC-Bench，一个面向医学图像分类中视觉语言模型（VLM）和医学视觉语言模型（Medical-VLM）的校准基准测试。该基准从模态/骨干网络/域偏移鲁棒性、校准策略与提示调优方法有效性、提示模板与随机种子变异性稳定性三个维度评估校准性能，覆盖8种骨干网络、3种医学模态（眼底影像、组织病理、胸部X光），在1638+个受控实验中对比多种校准方法，并提出Multi-Class Margin (MCM)正则化作为简单的训练时校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型研究主要聚焦于提升准确性，而模型校准（calibration）在医学领域长期被忽视。在临床安全关键场景中，可靠的置信度评估至关重要，因此需要构建一个系统化的校准基准来全面评估和改善医学VLM的校准表现。

Method: 提出了MVC-Bench校准基准，从三个轴评估校准：（i）对模态、骨干网络和域偏移的鲁棒性；（ii）校准策略与提示调优方法的有效性；（iii）在提示模板和随机种子变化下的稳定性。覆盖8种骨干网络、3种医学模态（眼底影像、组织病理、胸部X光），包含域内和域偏移设置，对比事后校准（post-hoc）、训练时校准（train-time）和零样本推理方法及6种提示调优方法。还提出Multi-Class Margin (MCM)正则化作为训练时校准方法。

Result: 在1638+个受控实验中，以准确率和Expected Calibration Error (ECE)为主要指标，辅以Maximum Calibration Error (MCE)和Adaptive Calibration Error (ACE)。提出的MCM正则化方法在域内设置的12个场景中有10个取得最低ECE，并在域偏移下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: MVC-Bench提供了一个结构化的评估框架，揭示了VLM和Medical-VLM校准不佳的根本原因，并为安全关键的医学工作流中的校准改进提供了可操作指导。MCM正则化作为一种简单有效的训练时校准方法，在域内和域偏移场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) and medical vision-language models (Medical-VLMs) requires calibrated confidence, particularly under realistic clinical conditions. However, existing efforts mainly focused on improving accuracy, leaving calibration in the medical domain underexplored. To this end, we propose MVC-Bench, a calibration-centric benchmark for medical image classification with VLMs and Medical-VLMs. MVC-Bench assesses the calibration across three axes: (i) robustness to modality, backbone, and domain shift (ii) effectiveness of calibration strategies and prompt-tuning methods (iii) stability under prompt-template and random-seed variations. The benchmark covers eight different backbones, three medical modalities, including fundus imaging, histopathology, and chest X-ray under in-domain and domain shift settings. It compares post-hoc calibration, train-time calibration, and zero-shot inference methods, together with six prompt-tuning methods. Across more than 1638 controlled experiments, we report accuracy and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as primary metrics, and further report results with complementary calibration measures, including Maximum Calibration Error (MCE) and Adaptive Calibration Error (ACE). We further investigate the underlying causes of miscalibration in VLMs and Medical-VLMs and propose a simple train-time calibration method, Multi-Class Margin (MCM) regularization, which achieves lowest ECE on 10 out of 12 settings in in-domain and remains competitive under domain shifts. Collectively, MVC-Bench provides a structured evaluation framework and actionable guidance for improving calibration in safety-critical medical workflows.

</details>


### [159] [Differentiable Jitter Correction using Deep Learning-based Image Quality Metric for Phase-Contrast Micro-CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27034)
*Junan Chen,Yiting Jia,Joscha Maier,Dominik John,Sami Wirtensohn,Imke Greving,Silja Flenner,Matthias Wieczorek,Julia Herzen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的全可微分抖动校正方法，用于X射线相衬显微CT成像。该方法利用深度学习图像质量指标直接从投影数据中估计并补偿每投影刚体抖动，无需预扫描无运动参考。采用梯度自聚焦策略适配平行束几何，训练紧凑3D卷积神经网络预测VIF分数，并结合仅作用于背景的空间选择性全变分正则项抑制伪高频结构。在多个同步辐射线站的生物样本实验中验证了方法的有效性和跨样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线相衬显微CT中，样本抖动会导致投影对齐误差，进而在重建图像中产生伪影并丢失精细结构细节。传统抖动校正方法通常需要无运动参考或依赖手工设计的图像质量指标，缺乏对不同伪影的鲁棒性，且难以端到端优化。

Method: 1) 将抖动校正建模为基于梯度的自聚焦优化问题，适配平行束CT几何；2) 构建可微分的重建-评估-优化闭环，从单个含抖动伪影的体数据中训练紧凑3D CNN预测视觉信息保真度(VIF)分数；3) 引入仅作用于图像背景的空间选择性总变分(TV)惩罚项，抑制优化过程中出现的虚假高频结构；4) 对候选目标函数进行受控基准测试，验证VIF指标对抖动伪影的敏感性。

Result: 在模拟和实验获取的投影数据上验证，该方法能够可靠恢复因抖动丢失的细微结构信息，并在不同形态学特征的生物样本上展现了良好的泛化能力，实验结果覆盖多个不同同步辐射线站获取的样本。

Conclusion: 集成流水线实现了无需预扫描参考的全可微分抖动校正，有效结合深度学习图像质量评价与正则化策略，在真实同步辐射数据上展现出稳定的校正性能和跨样本泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully differentiable jitter correction method for X-ray phase-contrast micro computed tomography using a deep learning-based image quality metric that estimates and compensates per-projection rigid jitter directly from the acquired projection data, without a pre-scan motion-free reference. The approach builds on a gradient-based auto-focus strategy adapted to parallel-beam geometry. A set of candidate objective functions is benchmarked in a controlled study, and the sensitivity of the visual information fidelity (VIF) metric to the jitter artifact is verified with the target phase-contrast data. To operate without a clean reference, a compact 3D convolutional neural network is trained to predict the VIF score from a single corrupted volume. A spatially selective total variation penalty applied exclusively to the image background is introduced to penalize spurious high-frequency structures that otherwise emerge during optimization. Experiments on biological specimens acquired at different synchrotron beamlines are conducted. Evaluation uses jitter motion applied to simulated and experimentally acquired projection data. The result confirms that the integrated pipeline reliably recovers fine structural detail lost due to jitter, with generalization demonstrated across morphologically distinct samples.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Person Human Motion Forecasting in Complex Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27039)
*Serdar Ozsoy,Lars Doorenbos,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCSD（Object-Conditioned Social Diffusion），一个条件扩散模型，将运动历史、多人互动和物体线索统一到一个框架中，用于在复杂场景中准确预测人群运动。在HiK和HOI-M3基准上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂场景中准确预测人群运动需要推理整个环境的过去和当前状态，但如何将物体信息和社交互动有效地纳入统一框架仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: OCSD使用物体条件机制在每个时间步调制去噪过程，实现细粒度的人-物推理；同时引入社交编码器建模场景中所有人之间的互动。该模型能自然处理变化的群体规模、复杂的社交互动，并支持对多种合理未来进行采样。

Result: 在Humans in Kitchens (HiK)基准上，两秒路径误差减少了121.5mm（31.3%）；在HOI-M3基准上减少了130.5mm（33.2%），同时能生成更逼真的长期预测，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: OCSD通过将物体条件化与社交建模统一到扩散框架中，显著提升了复杂场景下的人群运动预测精度和长期预测的真实性，验证了条件扩散模型在人-物-人交互建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting the movement of people in complex scenes requires reasoning over the past and present state of the entire environment. In this context, effectively incorporating object information and social interactions into a unified framework remains particularly challenging. To address this, we propose Object-Conditioned Social Diffusion (OCSD), a conditional diffusion model that integrates motion history, multi-person interactions, and object cues into a single framework. OCSD uses an object-conditioning mechanism that modulates denoising at every timestep, enabling fine-grained human-object reasoning, and a social encoder that models the interactions between all humans in the scene. As a result, our model naturally handles varying group sizes, complex social interactions, and supports sampling multiple plausible futures. Extensive experiments show that OCSD achieves state-of-the-art results on the Humans in Kitchens (HiK) and HOI-M3 benchmarks. It reduces the two-second path error by 121.5 mm (31.3%) on HiK and 130.5 mm (33.2%) on HOI-M3 compared to prior work, and produces more realistic long-term forecasts.

</details>


### [161] [Anatomy-Guided Foundation Model Adaptation with Within-Case Prototype Supervision for Standard Plane Detection in Fetal Ultrasound Blind Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27051)
*Yuzhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnatoProto，一个轻量级序列级框架，用于在低成本产科盲扫超声波视频中检测胎儿腹围标准平面。通过解剖加权空间池化、病例内原型损失、三级级联精炼和混合预测头四个组件，在极不平衡（正帧<3%）的帧分类任务上达到F1=67.72，超出最强基础模型基线13.20 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本产科盲扫中检测胎儿腹围标准平面是极度不平衡的帧分类问题：正帧占比不足3%、形成短连续片段，且现成的超声和视觉基础模型效果不佳。需要针对这一特定任务的轻量级解决方案。

Method: AnatoProto由四个组件构成：(i)解剖加权空间池化，利用nnU-Net腹部区域概率作为空间先验重加权冻结的BiomedCLIP补丁token；(ii)病例内原型损失，将每帧嵌入拉向同一扫查内正帧均值；(iii)三级级联精炼（帧→片段→病例级拒绝器），将预测单元从噪声帧提升到结构化片段；(iv)混合预测头，联合建模帧内稳定性和帧间边界转换以抑制边界假阳性。

Result: 在ACOUSLIC-AI基准上达到测试F1=67.72，超过最强基础模型基线FetalCLIP+PRS（54.52）13.20 F1，超过最强视频时间动作检测基线TriDet+PRS 15.76 F1。协同研究表明原型损失和解剖加权池化并非简单叠加：单独应用原型损失降低召回12点，但结合解剖加权池化则提升召回6.5点，存在符号翻转现象。

Conclusion: 验证了利用解剖先验和病例内结构可以显著提升极度不平衡的超声帧分类性能。协同研究的符号翻转揭示了原型损失与特征空间质量的深层交互：当特征被解剖池化引导到有意义区域时，原型更准确，从而发挥正向作用；反之孤立使用时原型不可靠导致召回下降。

Abstract: Detecting the fetal abdominal circumference standard plane in low-cost obstetric blind sweeps is a highly imbalanced frame-classification problem: positive frames account for under 3% of a sequence, form short contiguous segments, and are poorly handled by off-the-shelf ultrasound and vision foundation models. We propose AnatoProto, a lightweight sequence-level framework that adapts a frozen BiomedCLIP encoder to fetal blind sweeps through four components: (i) anatomy-weighted spatial pooling that uses nnU-Net abdominal-region probabilities as a spatial prior to reweight BiomedCLIP patch tokens, so frozen semantic features are aggregated onto anatomically meaningful regions; (ii) a within-case prototype loss that pulls each frame embedding toward the mean of positive frames of the same sweep, exploiting case-level structure unavailable at the frame level; (iii) a three-stage cascade refinement (frame->segment->case-level rejecter) that lifts the prediction unit from noisy frames to structurally-constrained segments; and (iv) a hybrid prediction head that jointly models per-frame stability and inter-frame boundary transitions to suppress boundary false positives. On the ACOUSLIC-AI benchmark, AnatoProto reaches a test F1 of 67.72, outperforming the strongest foundation-model baseline (FetalCLIP + PRS, F1 = 54.52) by +13.20 F1 and the strongest video temporal-action-detection baseline (TriDet + PRS) by +15.76 F1. A synergy study, backed by embedding geometry and paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, shows that the prototype loss and anatomy-weighted pooling are not additive: applied alone the prototype loss reduces recall by 12 points, but combined with anatomy-weighted pooling it increases recall by 6.5 points -- a sign-flip we trace to the accuracy of the within-case prototype.

</details>


### [162] [Video-OPSD: Exploiting Privileged Visual Evidence for On-Policy Self-Distillation in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27065)
*Ziyue Wang,Shiqi Huang,Weiwen Xu,Bihan Wen,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-OPSD，一种面向视频大语言模型的在线策略自蒸馏框架，利用视频中的特权视觉证据（关键帧）来构建自教师并引导token级知识蒸馏。实验表明其在多个backbone上优于标准OPSD，性能与GRPO相当但训练时间大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD作为一种有效的后训练范式在文本领域取得进展，但尚未在Video-LLM中得到充分探索。现有OPSD方法通常通过向教师上下文添加额外信息来构建特权教师，而视频推理本身提供了一种独特的特权监督来源——长视频存在大量时间冗余，仅少数关键帧即可支撑答案。利用这一特性可以构建更有信息量的教师监督。

Method: Video-OPSD包含两个核心组件：(1) Evidence-Grounded Self-Teacher：教师仅以标注的证据帧（关键帧）为条件进行推理，而学生仍然基于完整视频推理，从而使教师提供更具信息量的监督；(2) Evidence-Guided Token Optimization：根据每个推理token对特权视觉证据的依赖程度，自适应地为token级蒸馏分配权重，强调基于感知的推理。

Result: 在视频理解和推理基准上，Video-OPSD在多个backbone上持续优于标准OPSD，性能与GRPO相当但所需训练时间大幅减少，是一种有效且高效的后训练方法。

Conclusion: Video-OPSD揭示了视频数据中内在的特权视觉证据可作为OPSD自教师构建的独特优势来源，为Video-LLM的后训练提供了一种兼具效果与效率的新范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm that improves policy optimization through dense token-level supervision from a privileged self-teacher. Despite its promise, OPSD remains largely underexplored for Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing methods typically construct privileged teachers by augmenting their context with additional information while keeping the primary input unchanged for both teacher and student. Video reasoning, however, offers a distinct source of privileged supervision within the primary input itself: long videos contain substantial temporal redundancy, and only a small subset of frames provides the evidence necessary to answer a question. Building on this observation, we present $\textbf{Video-OPSD}$, an OPSD framework that exploits privileged visual evidence for both self-teacher construction and knowledge transfer. First, our Evidence-Grounded Self-Teacher conditions the teacher exclusively on annotated evidence frames while the student continues to reason over the complete video. This focused visual input enables the teacher to provide more informative supervision. Second, our Evidence-Guided Token Optimization adaptively weights token-level distillation according to each reasoning token's reliance on privileged visual evidence, thereby emphasizing perceptually grounded reasoning. Experiments across video understanding and reasoning benchmarks show that $\textbf{Video-OPSD}$ consistently improves upon Standard OPSD across multiple backbones and achieves performance comparable to GRPO while requiring substantially less training time, establishing an effective and efficient post-training approach for Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Classification: Task-Dependent Learnability under Privacy-Motivated Image Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27066)
*Leon Ranke,Wolfgang Hübner,Ronny Hug,Michael Arens,Jürgen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种计算感知的多任务评估协议，用于在模型训练中评估隐私增强技术（PETs）。该协议通过轻量级代理任务来补充图像分类评估的不足，证明不同PET即使在分类精度相似的情况下，在其他视觉任务上表现也可能差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PET评估过度依赖图像分类作为唯一指标，而分类主要反映语义可分性，对几何变形、空间布局或局部边界变化不敏感，无法代表通用视觉任务的表现。穷举下游任务评估又因需为每种PET变换和参数训练模型而计算代价过高。

Method: 提出一个计算感知的多任务评估协议，结合多个轻量级代理任务，针对视觉结构的互补方面（如空间、几何、局部边界等）进行评估，同时保持简单和快速的计算特性。协议适用于不可逆隐私变换、基于密钥的块级原语和学习型图像加密方案。

Result: 实验证明，在分类精度相似的情况下，不同PET在其他视觉任务上的表现可能存在显著差异，验证了当前PET评估仅依赖分类指标是不充分的。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调需要超越仅基于分类的评估范式，采用多任务、计算感知的协议来更全面地评估PET在通用视觉任务中的适用性和保护效果。

Abstract: Privacy-Enhancing Technologies (PETs) in computer vision often rely on noise or image perturbations to protect visual data while securely processing it, creating a trade-off between task performance and protection. This trade-off is commonly evaluated using image classification, which primarily captures semantic separability and remains robust despite significant geometric, spatial layout or local boundary alterations. As a result, it is too simplistic as a proxy for generic vision tasks. Exhaustive downstream-task evaluation, however, is computationally expensive because models must often be trained for each PET transformation and parameter setting. We therefore propose a compute-aware multi-task protocol for evaluating PETs in model training. It combines lightweight proxy tasks that target complementary aspects of visual structure while remaining simple and fast to compute. Across irreversible privacy transformations, key-based block primitives, and learnable image encryption schemes, we demonstrate that PETs with similar classification accuracy can differ substantially on other tasks. The outcomes highlight the need for PET evaluation protocols that move beyond classification-only reporting.

</details>


### [164] [SpatialCrafter: Single Image World Modeling with Generative 3D Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27073)
*Chuan Fang,Lingteng Qiu,Yixun Liang,Rui Chen,Kunming Luo,Zhaohua Zheng,Tongyuan Bai,Feipeng Tian,Zilong Dong,Zihan Zhou,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialCrafter 是一个两阶段的图像到场景生成框架，通过引入全局3D代理（global 3D proxy）来解决现有基于视频扩散模型方法在可探索场景生成中的幻觉、长期漂移和3D不一致问题。框架包含点锚定稀疏结构流模块（PaSS Flow）生成的几何一致3D代理，以及作为"生成式延迟细化器"使用的VDM来合成高频逼真细节。作者还构建了首个包含11.5万场景的图像到场景生成混合数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频扩散模型（VDM）的图像到场景生成方法依赖于不完整的条件信号（如稀疏点云或2D全景图），导致随机幻觉、长期漂移和次优的3D一致性。这在游戏、机器人和虚拟现实等应用中是需要解决的关键问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架SpatialCrafter：1）全局代理生成阶段，通过Point-anchored Sparse Structure (PaSS) Flow模块预测空间对齐且几何一致的3D代理；2）外观细化阶段，将VDM重框架为Generative Deferred Refiner，在代理定义的场景几何上合成高频逼真细节。同时引入Parallel Geometry Injection和Proxy-Aware Corruption训练策略以更好地融合代理与预训练VDM。此外构建了115K场景的大型混合数据集。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SpatialCrafter优于现有最先进方法，缓解了长期漂移问题，并且在快速相机运动和极端视点变化下保持鲁棒性和一致性。

Conclusion: 通过引入全局3D代理来分解"几何生成"与"外观细化"两个子任务，SpatialCrafter有效解决了可探索图像到场景生成中的核心挑战，并发布了代码、模型和新构建的数据集以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Explorable image-to-scene generation is essential for applications in gaming, robotics, and virtual reality. Existing methods based on video diffusion model (VDM) commonly rely on incomplete conditioning signals such as sparse point clouds or 2D panoramas, leading to stochastic hallucinations, long-term drifts and suboptimal 3D consistency. We present SpatialCrafter, a novel two-stage framework that addresses these issues by introducing a global 3D proxy for high-fidelity image-to-scene generation. Specifically, we decompose the generation process into global proxy generation and appearance refinement. For proxy generation, we propose a Point-anchored Sparse Structure~(PaSS) Flow module that predicts a spatially aligned and geometrically consistent 3D proxy. For appearance refinement, we re-frame the VDM as a Generative Deferred Refiner which synthesizes high-frequency photorealistic details upon proxy-defined scene geometry. To better integrate the proxy with the pre-trained VDM, we introduce Parallel Geometry Injection and Proxy-Aware Corruption training strategies, which improve robustness to proxy artifacts without disrupting the pretrained generative manifold. Furthermore, as no suitable dataset exists for this explorable scene generation task, we construct a new large-scale dataset of 115K scenes. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first hybrid dataset for image-to-scene generation. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that SpatialCrafter outperforms state-of-the-art methods, mitigates long-term drift, and remains robust and consistent under rapid camera motion and extreme viewpoint changes. Code, models, and the newly constructed dataset will be publicly released. See more at https://fangchuan.github.io/SpatialCrafter/.

</details>


### [165] [Automated 2D and 3D Segmentation of AMD and DME Lesions in OCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27095)
*Lucia Sundberg,Zhihao Zhao,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发和系统消融了四种OCT病变分割流程（AMD和DME的2D/3D变体），域内验证达到0.76-0.82的Dice分数及强体积/表面校准；通过消融建立了全体积、校准感知的采用标准，并识别集成组成为最一致的改进驱动因素。在无病变级标注的外部临床队列OLIVES上，使用以生物标志物AUROC、CST相关性和纵向一致性为核心的代理指标框架评估泛化能力，结果显示预测能追踪分布外临床生物标志物，但表现弱于域内——为临床工具提供了证据而非验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT是AMD和DME检测与监测的标准影像手段，但现有深度学习分割工作大多仅做域内验证，对跨采集协议的真实临床数据分布外泛化缺乏系统性检验。作者旨在通过系统消融和外部队列测试，弥补这一关键空白。

Method: 构建并系统消融四条病变分割流程（AMD与DME各自的2D和3D变体）；提出全体积、校准感知的采用标准以捕获切片级评估遗漏的机制；通过消融辨识集成组成为关键改进因素。泛化测试采用代理指标框架，包括生物标志物AUROC、中央子场厚度（CST）相关性和纵向一致性，应用于无病变级标注的外部临床队列OLIVES。

Result: 域内验证集上所有四条流程的Dice分数为0.76至0.82，体积与表面校准均强（r_vol、r_surf均≥0.97）。外部泛化测试中，模型预测在训练分布之外仍能追踪临床生物标志物，但关联强度弱于域内表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明预测量化病变负荷在分布外可追踪临床生物标志物，但当前证据不足以验证其作为临床工具的充分可靠性；域内强校准与外部弱关联形成对比，提示跨域泛化仍是该方向部署的首要挑战。

Abstract: Age-related macular degeneration (AMD) and diabetic macular edema (DME) are leading causes of vision loss, and optical coherence tomography (OCT) is the standard modality for detecting and monitoring the subtle lesions that drive treatment decisions. Most deep-learning segmentation work for OCT is validated only in-domain, leaving generalization to clinical data collected under different acquisition protocols largely untested. This work develops and systematically ablates four lesion-segmentation pipelines -- 2D and 3D variants for AMD and DME -- reaching Dice scores of 0.76 to 0.82 with strong volumetric and surface calibration (r vol, r surf greater than or equal to 0.97 across all four pipelines) on an in-domain validation set. The ablation process establishes a full-volume, calibration-aware adoption standard that catches mechanisms an ordinary slice-level evaluation would keep, and identifies ensemble composition as the most consistent driver of improvement. To test generalization, the models are evaluated on OLIVES, an external clinical cohort with no lesion-level ground truth, using a proxy-metric framework built around biomarker AUROC, central subfield thickness (CST) correlation, and longitudinal concordance. Predictions track clinical biomarkers outside the training distribution, though less strongly than in-domain -- evidence for, not validation of, automated lesion-burden tracking as a clinical tool.

</details>


### [166] [EditaLive! Unified Character Video Editing for Live Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27123)
*Zhiyuan Li,Chi-Man Pun,Peng-Tao Jiang,Bo Li,Xiaodong Cun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditaLive是一个面向实时流媒体场景的人物角色视频编辑框架，基于预训练图像动画模型Wan-Animate，通过参考帧编辑与视频重建、因果流式生成适配以及对齐的自rollout蒸馏策略，实现了保真面部表情的低延迟实时流式视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频编辑方法侧重场景级内容，而直播场景更关注人物主体。现有视频编辑方法存在两方面不足：一是可能引入面部表情不一致问题，二是依赖多次离线推理步骤，无法满足实时交互需求。

Method: (1) 基于预训练图像动画模型Wan-Animate，利用其自然解耦外观与运动的特性，通过参考帧编辑和视频重建方式，搭配自收集的CharEdit-50K数据集将其改造成指令式人物中心视频编辑模型；(2) 将模型从离线双向生成适配为因果流式生成；(3) 设计对齐的自rollout蒸馏策略，将模型压缩为两步采样器，其中固定RoPE和对齐强迫技术减少训练-推理差异，首帧保留的稀疏注意力过滤冗余历史信息以缓解外观漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明EditaLive在编辑性能上达到最先进水平，能够忠实保留面部表情，并实现低延迟的实时流式推理。

Conclusion: EditaLive成功解决了人物中心实时流式视频编辑的挑战，在保持编辑质量的同时实现了低延迟实时推理，为直播场景下的视频编辑提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conventional video editing primarily focuses on scene-level content, whereas live streaming places greater emphasis on the human subject. However, directly applying existing video-editing methods to human-centric live streaming remains challenging, as they may introduce facial-expression inconsistencies and typically depend on multiple offline inference steps, making them unsuitable for real-time interaction. We propose EditaLive, a novel framework for real-time streaming character video editing. In detail, we start from a pretrained image animation model (Wan-Animate), which naturally decouples appearance from motion, and repurpose it as the base model for instruction-based human-centric video editing by reference frame editing and video reconstruction via the collected CharEdit-50K dataset. Besides, we adapt the model from offline bidirectional to causal streaming generation, and design an aligned self-rollout distillation strategy that compresses the model into a two-step sampler, where fixed RoPE and align forcing reduce training--inference discrepancies, and first-frame preserved sparse attention filters redundant historical information to mitigate appearance drift. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditaLive delivers state-of-the-art editing performance with faithful preservation of facial expressions and low-latency real-time streaming inference.

</details>


### [167] [ReViCo: Unveiling the Limitations of VLMs in Visual Text Understanding via Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27154)
*Bojun Zhang,Junhong Liang,Feifei Zhai,Fengxian Ji,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReViCo基准测试，通过"视觉文本纠错"这一新颖任务评估视觉语言模型对图中文本的深层理解能力，实验发现即使最先进的VLM与人类表现之间仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型在通用视觉任务上表现优异，但在深入理解图像内文本方面仍存在明显不足。作者希望通过构建一个专门的基准来揭示这一短板，并推动更鲁棒的文本感知VLM的发展。

Method: ReViCo采用两种评测范式：(1)基于提示（prompt）的策略，(2)针对性的模型训练（targeted model training）。评测任务要求模型在真实世界图像中识别并修正文本错误，这需要对视觉文本与其周围视觉上下文之间的交互关系有深刻理解。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使表现最好的VLM与人类水平之间仍存在显著性能差距；进一步的错误分析表明，大多数模型难以准确感知视觉文本，导致频繁出现修正错误。

Conclusion: ReViCo通过揭示当前VLM在视觉文本理解上的能力缺口，为开发更鲁棒、更具文本感知能力的VLM提供了新的基准基础。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown great success in general visual tasks, yet they still struggle to deeply understand text within images. In this paper, we introduce ReViCo (Real Visual Correction), a benchmark designed to evaluate VLM text understanding through a novel task of visual text error correction. ReViCo challenges models to identify and fix text errors in real-world images, which requires a profound understanding of the interplay between visual text and its surrounding visual context. We benchmark various VLMs using two distinct paradigms: prompt-based strategy and targeted model training, both aimed at pushing the limits of current models. Our experiments reveal a striking performance gap between even the best VLMs and human, and further analysis also shows that most models struggle to accurately perceive the visual text, resulting in frequent correction errors. By highlighting these gaps, ReViCo provides a new benchmark foundation for developing more robust and text-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [168] [Magpie: Real-Time World Renderer for Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27168)
*Xiaoyu Zhan,Xinyu Wang,Xiaohong Zhang,Huanjie Zhu,Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Jiaxing Yu,Yanwen Guo,Dongjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Magpie是一个用于交互游戏的实时生成式世界渲染系统，它将游戏逻辑执行与视觉生成分离。游戏引擎负责解析玩家动作和维护世界状态，独立的渲染服务器根据引擎产生的白盒帧生成视觉输出，从而在保持游戏可设计性和可复现性的同时，减少早期游戏原型对完整视觉资产的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏开发依赖常规图形管线，高质量的视觉内容需要建模、材质创作、动画、光照、特效和运行时优化等多个环节，导致资产制作成本高昂并延长游戏原型的开发周期。虽然视频基础模型正在改变影视制作，但游戏不同于线性媒体，不仅需要连续且逼真的图像，还需要稳定可复现的游戏规则、对象状态和交互结果，因此需要一种将生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染的系统级方案。

Method: Magpie采用生成世界渲染与游戏逻辑解耦的架构：设计者在游戏引擎中定义场景和规则；运行时，游戏引擎解析玩家动作并维护世界状态，同时一个独立的Render Server根据引擎输出的白盒帧生成视觉内容。这种系统级实现路径将生成模型引入实时游戏渲染管线。

Result: Magpie提供了一个系统级的实现路径，可将生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染，保留了游戏玩法设计的可设计性和可复现性，并降低了早期游戏原型对完整视觉资产的依赖。

Conclusion: Magpie通过分离游戏执行与视觉生成，成功将生成式模型应用于实时游戏渲染领域，为游戏开发中高成本视觉资产生产问题提供了新的解决思路，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern game development relies heavily on conventional graphics pipelines. High-quality visual content requires modeling, material authoring, animation, lighting, effects, and runtime optimization, making asset production expensive and extending the development cycle of game prototypes. Recently, video foundation models are beginning to change film and video production, but games differ from linear media, they require not only continuous and realistic imagery, but also stable and reproducible gameplay rules, object states, and interaction outcomes. We present Magpie, a real-time generative world-rendering system for interactive games. Magpie separates gameplay execution from visual generation. Designers define scenes and rules in a game engine. At runtime, the Game Engine resolves player actions and maintains world state, while an independent Render Server generates visual output from white-box frames produced by the engine. Magpie provides a system-level implementation path for applying generative models to real-time game rendering. It preserves gameplay designability and reproducibility, and reduces the dependence of early game prototypes on complete visual assets.

</details>


### [169] [Ancient-Bench: A Comprehensive Multi-millennial, Multi-medium, and Multi-script Benchmark for Ancient Chinese Artifact Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27169)
*Hiuyi Cheng,Nuo Xu,Yuyi Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Wei Pan,Jing Zhang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Yihua Teng,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ancient-Bench，一个包含2,700张图像的古中文器物文字识别综合基准，横跨三千年文字演变、九类器物介质和七种历史书体，并定义了三种适应异质介质的标注标准。实验表明，现有的视觉语言模型和OCR专用模型在该任务上仍面临未解决的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古文字识别基准存在"碎片化"问题，表现为时间跨度有限、介质多样性不足、文字类型不完整，无法全面评估当前模型在古中文器物文字识别上的真实能力。

Method: 构建Ancient-Bench基准，包含2,700张图像，从三个维度设计：多千年（覆盖3,000年文字演变史）、多介质（涵盖九类器物类别）、多书体（涵盖七种历史字体形式）。同时针对异质介质定义三种标注标准：符号标准化、字符标准化和解析标准化，以保证跨介质的一致公平评估。

Result: 通过对通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）和OCR专用模型的大规模实验，发现古中文器物文字识别仍未被根本解决，主要挑战集中在异体字识别、特殊符号处理和模型幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 古中文器物文字识别是一个仍然开放且充满挑战的研究问题，Ancient-Bench为未来研究提供了统一的评估框架和标准化基准，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Ancient Chinese artifact text recognition is fundamental to heritage digitization, and benchmarks for ancient texts are essential for evaluating current model capabilities. However, existing benchmarks suffer from ''fragmentation'', manifested in limited temporal coverage, limited medium diversity, and incomplete script types. Therefore, we present Ancient-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 2,700 images for ancient Chinese artifact text recognition, featuring three dimensions: Multi-millennial (spanning 3,000 years of character evolution), Multi-medium (covering nine artifact categories), and Multi-script (encompassing seven historical script forms). To enable consistent and fair evaluation across heterogeneous media, we further define three annotation standards tailored to the medium-specific characteristics of ancient texts: symbol standardization, character standardization, and parsing standardization. Extensive experiments on Ancient-Bench covering general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and OCR-specialist models reveal that ancient Chinese artifact text recognition remains fundamentally unsolved, with persistent challenges in variant characters, specialized symbols, and hallucination. The dataset is available at https://github.com/SCUT-DLVCLab/Ancient_Bench.

</details>


### [170] [Temporal Sensitivity Analysis of Tessera Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27175)
*Julia Guerrero-Viu,Alex López-Cifuentes,Ignacio Pérez-Villar,Fabio Pacifici*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对地球观测基础模型Tessera进行了受控的时序敏感性研究，通过改变观测窗口（从全年到单日）评估其嵌入在土地利用/土地覆盖制图中的表现。结果显示嵌入的价值依赖任务类型：对依赖物候区分的作物类型提升显著（mIoU 58.3，比最佳从头训练模型高46%），对时序稳定的类别仅在完全监督下与从头训练模型相当，但在标签效率上始终优势明显。时间窗口缩短带来的退化是渐进且类依赖的，表明时间覆盖是可调节的成本而非固定前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多地球观测应用需要既精确又频繁更新的土地利用/土地覆盖地图，但最强的基础模型通常基于一整年观测构建嵌入。作者希望探究这些模型对观测时间窗口的敏感性，以评估它们能否支持近实时制图和更快的更新周期。

Method: 在冻结编码器的条件下，将Tessera模型在从全年到单日的不同观测窗口上重新计算嵌入，分别使用线性探针和UNet分割头进行评测，并以从头训练的模型作为基线，在LUCAS、DynamicEarthNet和PASTIS-R三个数据集上进行了基准对比。

Result: 在PASTIS-R（按物候区分作物类型）上嵌入模型mIoU达58.3，比最佳从头训练模型高约46%；在时序稳定的类别（如DynamicEarthNet和LUCAS中的森林）上，嵌入与从头训练模型仅在完全监督下持平，但嵌入模型始终更加标签高效。窗口从一年缩至一个月时，PASTIS-R精度损失39%，而DynamicEarthNet仅损失5%；单日嵌入在LUCAS上仍以3.4倍于随机水平的精度完成分类。

Conclusion: Tessera嵌入的时间价值高度依赖于任务的类别特性，时间覆盖应被视为可调节的成本而非固定前提，这为近实时制图和更快的土地利用/土地覆盖更新周期开辟了可能。

Abstract: Many Earth Observation applications need land-use/land-cover maps that are both precise and frequently updated, yet the strongest Earth Observation foundation models build their embeddings from a full year of observations. We present a controlled study of the temporal sensitivity of Tessera, one of these leading foundation models, for land-use/land-cover mapping. Keeping the encoder frozen, we recompute its embeddings over varying observation windows, from a full year down to a single day. We use them as inputs to a linear probe and a UNet segmentation head, benchmarking both of them against from-scratch networks on LUCAS, DynamicEarthNet, and PASTIS-R datasets. We show that the value of the embeddings is task-dependent. Where classes are separated by phenology, as for the crop types of PASTIS-R, they reach a mean Intersection-over-Union of $58.3$, about $46\%$ above the best from-scratch model. Where classes are temporally stable (e.g., forests in DynamicEarthNet and LUCAS), embedding-based and from-scratch models match only under full supervision. On both datasets, Tessera embeddings remain markedly more label-efficient. Degradation under shorter temporal windows is gradual and class-dependent. Contracting the window from one year to one month costs $39\%$ of the segmentation accuracy on PASTIS-R but only $5\%$ on DynamicEarthNet. Single-day embeddings still classify land cover in LUCAS at $3.4$ times the chance level. Our study shows that temporal coverage is therefore a tunable cost rather than a fixed prerequisite, opening regimes such as near-real-time mapping and faster land-use/land-cover refresh cycles.

</details>


### [171] [Parameter-Efficient pretrained-CT-to-MRI Transfer for Rectal Cancer Segmentation: Performance-Calibration Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27178)
*Aneesh Rangnekar,Jorge Tapias Gomez,Joseph O Deasy,Harini Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIFT，一种用于直肠癌MRI分割的Swin V2预训练模型与参数高效/肿瘤感知微调框架。通过四种配置（全微调、解码器压缩、LoRA、LoRA解码器集成）在247例测试集上评估，结果显示SWIFTe减少70.1%参数且提高肿瘤检出率，LoRA变体保留性能同时大幅减少可训练参数，LoRA解码器集成获得最佳校准效果，但绝对校准误差仍显示残余校准不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直肠癌MRI精确分割对自适应放疗和肿瘤反应评估至关重要，但实际部署还要求计算效率高和具有信息量、经过校准的不确定性估计。现有方法在这些维度上往往顾此失彼，缺乏同时兼顾几何精度、效率与概率校准的系统性研究。

Method: 使用在10,444个公开3D CT体素上通过DINOv2风格目标预训练的Swin V2编码器，适配到T2加权MRI，构建四种累积配置：全微调（SWIFT）、解码器压缩（SWIFTe）、低秩适配（SWIFTe-LoRA）、四成员LoRA-解码器集成（SWIFTe-LDE4）。在247例单中心1.5T/3T GE扫描仪测试集上评估几何精度、肿瘤检测、影像组学一致性和概率校准。

Result: SWIFTe比SWIFT减少70.1%参数（72.8M→21.8M），肿瘤检出率从89.9%提高到93.9%，表面DSC略降（0.61 vs 0.62）但影像组学一致性改善。SWIFTe消融实验显示移除肿瘤感知增强使检出率降至89.9%但DSC升至0.64，体现检测-边界权衡。SWIFTe-LoRA仅用SWIFTe 14.6%可训练参数保持类似分割性能。SWIFTe-LDE4在温度缩放后获得最低校准误差（ECE=0.217，Brier=0.222），但绝对ECE仍显示残余校准不足。VoCo检查点验证了跨预训练初始化的稳健性。

Conclusion: SWIFT系列展示了参数高效微调在直肠癌MRI分割中的效率-精度-校准权衡空间：压缩模型可提升检测率并节省资源，LoRA集成改善概率校准但绝对误差仍提示需进一步改进校准方法。研究结果表明检测性能与边界分割准确度之间存在固有权衡，且模式在多种预训练初始化上具有稳健性。

Abstract: Accurate rectal cancer segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for adaptive radiotherapy and tumor response assessment, but deployment also requires computational efficiency and informative, calibrated uncertainty estimates. We therefore introduce SWIFT, a SWin pretrained model wIth parameter-eFficient and Tumor-aware fine-tuning for rectal cancer segmentation. A Swin V2 encoder pretrained on 10,444 public 3D CT volumes using a DINOv2-style objective was adapted to T2-weighted MRI through four cumulative configurations: full fine-tuning (SWIFT), decoder compression (SWIFTe), low-rank adaptation (SWIFTe-LoRA), and a four-member LoRA-decoder ensemble (SWIFTe-LDE4). Geometric accuracy, tumor detection, radiomic agreement, and probability calibration were evaluated on a held-out 247-case test set from a single-institution cohort acquired using 1.5 or 3 Tesla GE scanners. Compared with SWIFT, SWIFTe reduced total parameters by 70.1% (from 72.8M to 21.8M) and increased tumor detection rate from 89.9% to 93.9%, while achieving a slightly lower median surface DSC (0.61 versus 0.62) and improved radiomic agreement. In a separate SWIFTe ablation, removing tumor-aware augmentation reduced detection from 93.9% to 89.9% but increased surface DSC from 0.61 to 0.64, demonstrating a detection-boundary-agreement trade-off. SWIFTe-LoRA used 14.6% of SWIFTe's trainable parameters while retaining similar segmentation performance. SWIFTe-LDE4 achieved the lowest calibration errors among the four configurations after temperature scaling (expected calibration error, 0.217; Brier score, 0.222), although the absolute expected calibration error indicates residual miscalibration. Similar efficiency-calibration patterns were observed using the public VoCo checkpoint, supporting robustness across pretrained initializations rather than external clinical generalizability.

</details>


### [172] [SSMB: Self-Supervised Local Feature Detection under Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27181)
*Zhenjun Zhao,Fabio Bellavia,Wenting Wang,Fan Zhu,Jiajun Wu,Suryansh Kumar,Mingqiang Wei,Haoang Li,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSMB是一种无需去模糊、完全自监督的稀疏关键点检测器，专为运动模糊图像设计。它通过局部判别性增强模块恢复全局特征混合后的细粒度判别力，并采用两阶段训练策略（几何形状预训练+真实模糊图像对上的模糊感知训练），在关键点检测、图像匹配、相对位姿估计和视觉定位等任务上均达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动模糊会扭曲局部图像结构并降低特征定位的重复性，导致关键点检测极具挑战。现有方法要么依赖计算昂贵的"先去模糊再检测"流水线（可能引入重建伪影），要么学习在清晰图像上回归手工设计的关键点位置（仅反映手工检测器的假设，而非模糊下真正可重复的特征）。因此需要一种既不依赖手工检测器也不依赖外部伪标签的、专门针对模糊图像的通用关键点检测方法。

Method: SSMB采用无需去模糊的自监督框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 局部判别性增强（LDE）模块，用于在全局特征混合后恢复细粒度的局部判别能力；(2) 两阶段训练：第一阶段在合成几何形状上进行几何预训练，仅从渲染的几何信息引导空间判别性关键点检测，无需外部检测器；第二阶段在真实清晰-模糊图像对上进行模糊感知训练，通过多组件自监督目标函数同时约束跨域一致性、几何对齐和空间覆盖。

Result: 在运动模糊条件下的关键点检测、图像匹配、相对位姿估计和视觉定位等多项任务上进行的大量评估表明，SSMB在稀疏关键点检测器中建立了新的最先进性能，在所有任务中一致地超过了监督式和自监督式基线方法。

Conclusion: SSMB证明了通过纯自监督的两阶段训练策略（合成几何预训练+真实模糊感知训练），可以构建出无需去模糊、不依赖手工检测器或外部伪标签的高鲁棒性模糊图像关键点检测器，为模糊场景下的视觉定位与匹配提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Keypoint detection under motion blur remains a significant challenge, as blur distorts local image structure and degrades the repeatability of feature localization. Existing approaches either rely on computationally expensive deblur-then-detect pipelines that may introduce restoration artifacts, or learn to regress the image positions of handcrafted keypoints extracted on sharp images, which reflects the assumptions of the handcrafted detector rather than what is truly repeatable under blur. We present SSMB, a deblur-free, self-supervised keypoint detector for motion-blurred images that requires neither handcrafted detectors nor external pseudo-labels. SSMB introduces the Local Discriminability Enhancement (LDE) module, which restores fine-grained local discriminability after global feature mixing. Training is performed in two stages. First, geometric pretraining on synthetic shapes bootstraps spatially discriminative keypoint detection without any external detector, just from the rendered geometry. Second, blur-aware training on real sharp-blur image pairs learns blur-invariant detection through a multi-component self-supervised objective that enforces cross-domain consistency, geometric alignment, and spatial coverage. Extensive evaluations on keypoint detection, image matching, relative pose estimation, and visual localization under motion blur demonstrate that SSMB establishes a new state-of-the-art among sparse keypoint detectors, consistently outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines across all tasks. Code, models, and datasets will be publicly available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [173] [Unsupervised Adaptation of 3D CT Foundation Models for 3D CBCT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27190)
*Gauthier Miralles,Loic Le Folgoc,Vincent Jugnon,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于冗余减少特征对齐的无监督域自适应（UDA）框架，用于CBCT的3D分割，无需目标域标注或推理时自适应。框架与架构无关，可适配CNN和ViT基础模型，在两个CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准上超越现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略，并公开代码、模型及肝脏分割标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT的3D分割在介入和放射治疗中至关重要，但面临两大挑战：标注CBCT数据稀缺，以及CBCT与诊断CT之间存在显著的模态域差异（由采集物理效应和血管对比度内容差异导致），这限制了跨模态模型迁移的有效性。

Method: 提出一种基于冗余减少特征对齐的无监督域自适应框架，在特征空间进行跨域桥接。该框架是架构无关的，能无缝适配CNN和ViT两类基础分割模型，且不需要目标域标注或推理时自适应。

Result: 在两个CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准（介入血管手术场景和放疗场景）上验证，结果表明即使大规模预训练分割网络也需显式特征空间桥接才能在跨采集模态上泛化，所提方法一致优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略。

Conclusion: 该工作证明特征空间的显式对齐对跨模态CBCT分割至关重要，并提供公开的肝脏分割标注、代码、训练模型和权重以支持可复现性和后续基准测试。

Abstract: Accurate 3D segmentation of cone-beam CT (CBCT) is critical for interventional and radiation therapy applications, yet it remains limited by two compounding challenges: the scarcity of annotated CBCT data and the large domain shift from diagnostic CT. Interventional CBCT exhibits fundamental modality differences from conventional CT, driven by acquisition and physics effects as well as contrast-specific vascular content, thereby limiting effective cross-modality model transfer. We propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework based on redundancy-reducing feature alignment, enabling 3D CBCT segmentation with no target-domain annotations or inference-time adaptation. Our framework is architecture-agnostic, seamlessly adapting both CNN-based and ViT-based foundation models. We evaluate our method on two challenging CT-CBCT liver segmentation benchmarks: one for interventional vascular procedures and one for radiation therapy, demonstrating that even large-scale pretrained segmentation networks require explicit feature-space bridging to generalize across acquisition modalities, and that our approach consistently outperforms existing pretrained foundation model and UDA strategies. To support reproducibility and benchmarking, we release the liver segmentations for a public CBCT dataset, along with the code, trained models, and weights.

</details>


### [174] [Vision-centric generative AI models: A software-hardware perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27199)
*Eleni Tselepi,Cristian Sestito,Shady Agwa,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉生成式AI应在硬件约束下进行软硬件协同设计，主张"正确的模型运行在正确的硬件上服务于正确的应用"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成式AI虽以大规模数据中心文本-图像应用闻名，但在自动驾驶、农业传感器和移动设备等硬件严格受限的边缘场景同样需要此类模型。然而当前进展主要由输出质量驱动，硬件被动适应不断增长的模型需求，导致边缘部署缺乏可持续性和可及性。

Method: 量化评估了各生成模型家族在不同加速器平台上的参数成本与能效，并将四类生成模型家族映射到七个真实应用领域，以系统分析模型与硬件之间的匹配关系，进而提出软硬件协同设计框架。

Result: 揭示出硬件演化被动跟随模型规模增长的现状，论证了软硬件协同设计而非单纯追求输出质量的必要性，并通过模型-硬件-应用的映射关系展示可持续部署的可行路径。

Conclusion: 应转变设计范式，将部署约束前置到设计流程的起点，通过软硬件协同设计使视觉生成式AI能够在更广泛的平台上实现可持续、可及的部署。

Abstract: Vision generative artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as one of the most rapidly advancing areas of deep learning. The explosion of multimodal models has made them widely associated with text-to-image applications running on large datacentres. However, vision generative models are equally needed in applications that operate under strict hardware constraints at the edge, including autonomous vehicles, agricultural sensors, and mobile devices. In this Perspective, we argue that progress in vision generative AI has been driven by output quality, with hardware evolving reactively to accommodate growing model demands. We quantify the parameter cost and energy efficiency of these models across a range of accelerator platforms, and map four generative model families against seven real-world application domains. Finally, we advocate a software-hardware co-design approach, where deployment constraints are considered from the start of the design process, ensuring that the "right model" runs on the "right hardware" to serve the "right application", making generative AI deployment sustainable and accessible across a much broader range of platforms.

</details>


### [175] [PACE: A Unified Condense-and-Extract Paradigm for Fast VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27206)
*Junjie Liu,Shengyuan Ye,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个无需训练的推理加速框架，通过统一的Condense-and-Extract范式同时加速视觉编码器和LLM阶段，在仅使用10%视觉token的情况下保留93.8%的原始性能，实现3.1倍的TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）随着视觉token数量激增，推理成本急剧上升。现有视觉token剪枝方法存在两个根本性局限：一是多数方法仅在视觉编码器之后操作，无法优化视觉编码阶段的大量延迟；二是在严格的token预算下，难以同时保留整体视觉上下文和细粒度细节，导致性能下降。

Method: PACE框架包含两个阶段：1）Condense阶段，自适应像素压缩器（APC）在编码前评估视觉信息密度，自适应地下采样冗余输入，减少编码器计算量同时保留全局上下文和关键视觉线索；2）Extract阶段，动态双注意力提取器（DDAE）通过融合来自编码器的内部视觉信号和来自LLM的语义信号，选择性地保留视觉token，保护任务关键细节。

Result: 将PACE集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B后，模型在仅使用10%视觉token的情况下保留了93.8%的原始性能，并且在首个token生成时间（TTFT）上实现了3.1倍的加速。

Conclusion: PACE通过统一的Condense-and-Extract范式，同时优化了视觉编码阶段和LLM推理阶段的效率，证明了在严格token预算下兼顾全局上下文与细粒度细节的可行性，为VLM推理加速提供了有效的无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate exceptional visual reasoning capabilities, yet their inference costs escalate rapidly with the proliferation of visual tokens. Existing visual token pruning methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. First, most approaches operate exclusively post-vision encoder, leaving the substantial latency of the visual encoding phase unoptimized. Second, under strict token budgets, these methods often fail to jointly preserve holistic visual contexts and fine-grained details, leading to performance degradation. To address these bottlenecks, we propose PACE (Pixel-Adaptive Condense and Extract), a training-free inference framework that accelerates both the vision encoder and the Large Language Model (LLM) via a unified Condense-and-Extract paradigm. During the Condense stage, an Adaptive Pixel Compressor (APC) evaluates visual information density prior to encoding, adaptively downsampling redundant inputs, curtailing encoder computation while preserving global context and essential visual cues. In the Extract stage, a Dynamic Dual-Attention Extractor (DDAE) selectively retains visual tokens via a fusion of internal visual signals from the encoder and semantic signals from the LLM, safeguarding task-critical details. By integrating PACE into Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the model retains 93.8% of its original performance while utilizing only 10% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.1x speedup in time to first token (TTFT). Our code is available at https://github.com/jjL357/PACE.

</details>


### [176] [CODE: Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression for Open World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27214)
*Hao Xu,Zhaoning Shi,Hehe Jin,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CODE是一个统一的推理时框架，通过跨模态联合置信度校准、不确定性引导的通用对象性增强和基于置信度边界的动态异常抑制三个互补组件，解决开放世界目标检测中跨模态语义歧义和未知对象过抑制的问题。在Real-World Detection基准上，使用OWL-ViT L/14骨干网络，Task 1中U-mAP达21.7，K-mAP达40.8，分别超越此前最优方法2.6和2.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于多模态基础模型的开放世界目标检测（OWOD）面临两个核心挑战：一是单向的文本到视觉匹配导致语义歧义；二是刚性异常惩罚机制会过度抑制位于已知类决策边界附近的未知对象，从而损害对未知类别的检测能力。

Method: CODE框架包含三个互补组件：(1) 跨模态联合置信度校准，通过注入全局视觉原型来校准文本驱动的已知类别预测；(2) 不确定性引导的通用对象性增强，利用局部视觉响应的分类犹豫程度来强化潜在未知对象；(3) 基于置信度边界的动态异常抑制，用边界感知的调整取代刚性抑制，从而保留模糊的分布外实例。

Result: 在Real-World Detection基准上，使用OWL-ViT L/14骨干网络，CODE在Task 1中实现了21.7的U-mAP和40.8的K-mAP，分别超过了此前最先进方法2.6和2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: CODE通过推理时的多组件协同校准，有效缓解了跨模态语义歧义和未知对象过度抑制问题，显著提升了开放世界目标检测在已知和未知类别上的综合性能，验证了动态、自适应机制相比刚性策略在开放世界检测中的优越性。

Abstract: Open World Object Detection (OWOD) built on multimodal foundation models often suffers from semantic ambiguity caused by unidirectional text-to-vision matching, while rigid outlier penalties may over-suppress unknown objects near known-class decision boundaries. We propose CODE (Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression), a unified inference-time framework with three complementary components. Cross-Modal Joint Confidence Calibration injects global visual prototypes to calibrate text-driven known-class predictions. Uncertainty-Guided Universal Objectness Enhancement measures classification hesitation from local visual responses to strengthen potential unknown objects. Dynamic Outlier Suppression via Confidence Margin replaces rigid suppression with a margin-aware adjustment that preserves ambiguous out-of-distribution instances. Experiments on the Real-World Detection benchmark demonstrate that, with the OWL-ViT L/14 backbone, CODE achieves 21.7 U-mAP and 40.8 K-mAP in Task 1, surpassing the previous state of the art by 2.6 and 2.3 points, respectively.

</details>


### [177] [DINOcular: Self-Supervised Visuospatial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27226)
*Farkhat Almukhamedov,Sami Azirar,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督框架，从RGB-D观测中学习联合视觉空间表征，通过深度几何先验与视觉主干网络的块间与块内融合，在3D几何基准上超越同类方法，同时保持语义迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉基础模型几乎只使用RGB图像训练，但许多具身系统拥有显式深度传感，可提供单目输入无法恢复的几何信息。现有方法未能有效利用深度信息来学习统一的视觉空间表征。

Method: 采用自监督框架，将深度导出的几何先验与视觉主干网络相结合，通过块间（inter-patch）和块内（intra-patch）两种融合方式，高效编码外观信息与空间结构。

Result: 在多个3D几何基准上优于同规模先前方法，并在RGB-D语义分割探测任务中保持竞争力，兼顾3D感知能力与语义可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效整合RGB外观与深度几何信息，在提升3D感知能力的同时不牺牲语义表征质量，为具身智能系统中视觉表征学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a self-supervised framework for learning joint visuospatial representations from RGB-D observations. While modern vision foundation models are trained almost exclusively on RGB images, many embodied systems have access to explicit depth sensing, which provides geometric information that monocular inputs cannot recover. Our method integrates depth-derived geometric priors with a visual backbone through inter-patch and intra-patch fusion, enabling the model to encode both appearance and spatial structure efficiently. The resulting representation shows promising improvements on 3D awareness while preserving semantic transfer: it outperforms prior methods of comparable scale on multiple 3D geometry benchmarks, and remains competitive when probed for standard RGB-D semantic segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [178] [UniFLM: United Segmentation and Measurement on Fetal Limb Ultrasonic Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27240)
*Zeen Zhou,Qiuhua Chen,Xiaojun Cao,Changmao Chen,Chao Sun,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了FLB胎儿肢体骨骼数据集（涵盖肱骨、股骨、胫腓骨、桡尺骨），并提出UniFLM统一框架，通过语义感知跳跃连接、正样本采样策略和点回归映射模块，实现胎儿长骨的自动跨平面分割与骨长测量，在精度和泛化性上优于现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声检查对评估肢体发育和检测先天性异常至关重要，但现有AI模型因缺乏高质量标注数据和统一的多长骨框架而常忽略致死性骨骼发育异常；此外，通用分割模型难以应对超声图像固有的噪声和语义鸿沟问题。

Method: 1) 构建FLB数据集，含肱骨、股骨、胫腓骨、桡尺骨的高质量标注；2) 提出UniFLM统一框架，包含Semantic-Aware Skip Connection模块以弥合编码器与解码器的语义差距，Positive Sampling策略自适应过滤噪声并提取关键语义信息；3) 引入Point Regression Mapping模块学习临床医师标注模式以实现精确骨长测量。

Result: 在FLB数据集上的大量实验表明，UniFLM在胎儿长骨评估中取得了优于当前SOTA模型的精度和更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFLM通过统一框架有效解决了胎儿长骨分割与测量中的噪声和语义鸿沟难题，为临床胎儿骨骼发育评估提供了更可靠的自动化工具，未来有望在更多超声应用场景中扩展。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound examination is crucial for assessing fetal limb development and detecting congenital anomalies. However, existing artificial intelligence models often overlook fetal lethal skeletal dysplasias due to the lack of high-quality annotated data and a unified framework for multiple long bones. Moreover, generic segmentation models struggle with the inherent noise and semantic gaps in ultrasound images. To address these challenges, we construct the Fetal Limb Bones (FLB) dataset, comprising high-quality annotations for the humerus, femur, tibia-fibula, and radius-ulna. Furthermore, we propose UniFLM, a unified framework for automatic cross-plane segmentation and measurement. UniFLM incorporates a Semantic-Aware Skip Connection module to bridge the semantic gap between encoder and decoder features, and a Positive Sampling strategy to adaptively filter noise and extract essential semantic information. Finally, a Point Regression Mapping module is introduced to learn clinician annotation patterns for precise bone length measurement. Extensive experiments conducted on the FLB dataset demonstrate that the proposed UniFLM achieves superior accuracy and enhanced generalization capabilities in fetal long bone assessment compared to current state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [179] [Sidecar: Training-Free Semantic Reuse for Character-Consistent Free-form Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27280)
*Sibo Dong,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Sidecar，一种即插即用的语义增强模块，用于保持自由形式故事生成中角色身份的一致性问题。它从首次介绍中保留实体级信息，并注入到后续提示嵌入中，无需额外训练或修改基础扩散模型的架构，在FreeStoryBench上显著提升提示-图像对齐和角色一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由形式故事生成中，角色仅在首次出现时被完整描述，后续提示往往只使用类型级提及或代词引用，丢失了重要的身份相关语义，导致角色一致性难以维持。现有方法无法有效解决这种自然叙事场景下语义缺失带来的身份漂移问题。

Method: 提出Sidecar模块，它是一个即插即用的语义增强组件：从初始描述中提取并保留实体级（entity-level）信息，将缺失的语义注入到后续提示的嵌入表示中。该方法无需任何额外训练，也不修改基础扩散模型（如SDXL和FLUX）的架构。

Result: 在FreeStoryBench基准上，Sidecar在多个基于SDXL和FLUX的基线模型上一致地提升了提示-图像对齐度和角色一致性，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Sidecar作为一种轻量级、即插即用的语义增强方案，有效解决了自由形式叙事中角色一致性维护的难题，证明了无需修改模型架构或额外训练即可通过语义注入实现显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Visual storytelling requires generating images that follow a narrative while preserving consistent character identities across frames. In free-form story generation, a character is fully described only when first introduced and is later referred to by a type-level mention or pronoun. Although this setting better reflects natural storytelling, later prompts may omit important identity-related semantics, making character consistency more difficult to maintain. We propose \textbf{Sidecar}, a plug-and-play semantic augmentation module that preserves entity-level information from the initial description and injects the missing semantics into later prompt embeddings. Sidecar requires no additional training and does not modify the architecture of the base diffusion model. Experiments on FreeStoryBench show that Sidecar consistently improves prompt-image alignment and character consistency across multiple SDXL- and FLUX-based baselines, with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [180] [TADP: Task-Aware Deformable Prediction for Single-Stage 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27282)
*Su Wang,Yaochen Li,Min Yang,Jiaohao Nie,Chang Liu,Yuehu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于单阶段3D目标检测的任务感知可变形预测（TADP）方法，通过三重特征细化聚合模块、多尺度特征聚合块和即插即用的任务感知变形头来解决不同任务共用相同特征空间的问题。在KITTI数据集上，该方法在车辆检测任务上达到了80.91%的mAP，超越了众多现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数单阶段3D目标检测器使用相同提取的特征来完成不同子任务（如分类、定位等），但将所有任务的特征投影到同一公共空间是次优的，因为不同任务对特征的需求各不相同。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 三重特征细化聚合模块，用于自适应提取三级特征；(2) 多尺度特征聚合块，以尺度感知的方式融合多尺度特征；(3) 即插即用的任务感知变形头，通过三种不同的变形模块感知各任务的重点和交互关系，并对每个任务的预测进行变形处理。

Result: 实验证明所提出的变形头在其他检测方法上也表现良好。在KITTI数据集上，汽车类别的mAP达到80.91%，超越了KITTI基准上的许多最先进方法。

Conclusion: 任务感知的可变形预测策略能够有效解决单阶段3D检测中多任务特征共享的冲突问题，且变形头具良好泛化性，可以即插即用地应用于其他检测方法。

Abstract: Most single-stage 3D object detectors complete different tasks with the same extracted features. Nevertheless, it is impossible to project features into a common space that is adaptive for all the tasks. We present a novel task-aware deformable prediction (TADP) method for single-stage 3D object detection to solve this problem. Firstly, a triple feature refinement aggregation module is designed to extract three-level features adaptively. Additionally, we design the multi-scale feature aggregation block to fuse multi-scale features in a scale-aware manner. Finally, the prediction of each task is deformed with the designed plug-and-play task-aware deformation head. It can percept the emphasis and interaction of each task. We also designed three different deformation modules. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed deformation head shows good results on other detection methods. The experimental results on the KITTI dataset demonstrate that the car mAP is 80.91%, surpassing many state-of-the-art methods on the KITTI benchmark.

</details>


### [181] [Detection of Christmas tree plantations from high-resolution aerial imagery. A case study in the French Morvan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27290)
*Francesca Razzano,Emanuele Dalsasso,Adrien Baysse-Lainé,Silvia Liberata Ullo,Gilda Schirinzi,Jocelyn Chanussot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将圣诞树种植园遥感制图定义为稀有目标语义分割问题，提出硬负样本挖掘（HNM）策略以应对其高密度种植、短轮伐周期、与周围植被视觉混淆及严重类别不平衡等挑战。基于DeepLabV3+ResNet-34模型在2020测试集取得IoU 0.733、F1 0.846，HNM将PR-AUC从0.204提升至0.913；时间迁移和大型验证进一步验证了框架的有效性与任务内在难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圣诞树种植园在经济上具有重要意义，但在遥感领域几乎未被探索。由于其高种植密度、短轮伐周期、与周围植被的视觉混淆、仅单年参考年份有密集标签，以及景观尺度上严重的类别不平衡，使其描绘极具挑战性。现有深度学习方法多面向森林、一般性种植园或果园设计，未明确针对圣诞树种植园的结构特殊性和难分负样本混淆问题。

Method: 本文提出三项贡献：(i) 将圣诞树种植园制图框架化为稀有目标语义分割问题；(ii) 引入硬负样本挖掘（HNM）策略以增强对混淆背景模式的判别能力；(iii) 在监督测试、时间迁移和大规模验证三个互补层面评估所提框架。采用DeepLabV3+ResNet-34作为最佳模型架构。

Result: 在2020测试集上，最佳模型DeepLabV3+ResNet-34取得IoU 0.733、F1 0.846。HNM大幅改善精确率-召回率行为，将PR曲线下面积从0.204提升至0.913。时间推断表现出有意义的迁移能力：2017/2018年IoU/F1为0.751/0.858，2023年为0.691/0.817。大规模验证显示圣诞树种植园仅占评估区域极小比例，2017/2018年为1,498.4公顷（1.72%），2023年为1,782.2公顷（2.04%），共87,309.4公顷。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将圣诞树种植园制图作为稀有目标分割问题并结合硬负样本挖掘的有效性，同时在时间迁移和大规模验证层面展现了框架的鲁棒性。结果也凸显了该任务固有的难度，圣诞树种植园在景观中占据极小比例，需要专门针对类别不平衡和难分负样本的策略。

Abstract: Christmas tree plantations are economically relevant, yet a largely unexplored application domain in Remote Sensing (RS). Their delineation is challenging because of high planting density, short rotation cycles, visual confusion with surrounding vegetation, the availability of dense labels for one reference year only, and severe class imbalance at the landscape scale. Although Deep Learning (DL) methods have shown strong potential for vegetation mapping, existing approaches are typically designed for forests, generic plantation systems, or orchards, and do not explicitly address the structural specificity and hard-negative confusion that characterize Christmas tree plantations. In response to these challenges, this work makes three main contributions: (i) it frames Christmas tree plantation mapping as a distinct rare-target semantic segmentation problem; (ii) it introduces a Hard Negative Mining (HNM) strategy to improve discrimination against confusing background patterns; and (iii) it evaluates the proposed framework across complementary levels, including supervised testing, temporal transfer, and large-scale validation. On the 2020 test set held out, the best model, DeepLabV3 with a ResNet-34 encoder, achieves an IoU of 0.733 and an F1-score of 0.846. HNM substantially improves precision-recall behavior, increasing the area under the precision-recall curve from 0.204 to 0.913. Temporal inference further shows meaningful transferability, reaching IoU/F1 values of 0.751/0.858 on 2017/2018 and 0.691/0.817 on 2023. Large-scale validation further highlights the intrinsic difficulty of the task, as Christmas tree plantations occupied only a very small fraction of the extent of the common evaluation, corresponding to 1,498.4 ha (1.72\%) in 2017/2018 and 1,782.2 ha (2.04\%) in 2023 out of 87,309.4 ha in total.

</details>


### [182] [R2M-Bench: Evaluating Revisit Memory via Relative Consistency in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27328)
*Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Rui Chen,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qishuai Wen,Li Niu,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R2M-Bench基准，通过在同一条rollout中将回访帧对与时间匹配的非回访帧对及短程帧对进行相对比较，消除原始回访相似度对渲染稳定性、重复内容和运动失败的敏感性，从而有效度量视频世界模型的可观测回访记忆一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型的记忆评估存在根本歧义：首次访问帧与回访帧的高相似度可能仅因rollout变化很小，而非模型真正记住了场景。这种歧义使绝对回访分数受渲染稳定性、重复内容和运动失败的影响，需要一种能区分"回访特有的记忆一致性"与"一般时间稳定性"的评估方法。

Method: 提出R2M-Bench基准，对每个检测到的回访对，从同一条rollout中配两个对照组：一个间隔匹配的非回访对（度量一般时间稳定性）和一个短程对（估计短时一致性）。由此计算MemoryGain（MG，回访相对于时间基线的优势）和Normalized Memory Ratio（NMR，将该优势按短程到基线的动态范围归一化）。基准包含100个参考场景×3条离开-返回轨迹=300个实例，评估外观保真度、场景与物体同一性、局部几何和持久状态。

Result: 在7个动作条件视频世界模型上，Overall NMR与人类一致性判断的Spearman相关系数为ρ=0.547（95% CI [0.45,0.63]）；其与生成运动的模型内相关幅度为0.072，远低于原始回访相似度的0.207，表明相对校准显著减少了慢动作捷径。DreamX-World-Memo取得了最高的Overall NMR。

Conclusion: 同rollout相对校准是一种实用且有效的方法，能够将回访特有的记忆一致性与一般时间稳定性区分开来，为视频世界模型的记忆评估提供了更可靠的标准。

Abstract: High similarity between first-visit and return frames does not necessarily show that a video world model remembered the scene; the intervening rollout may simply have changed very little. This ambiguity makes absolute revisit scores sensitive to rendering stability, repetitive content, and failed motion. We introduce \emph{R2M-Bench} (\textbf{R}elative \textbf{R}evisit \textbf{M}emory Benchmark), a benchmark of observable revisit-selective consistency. For every detected return, R2M-Bench compares the revisit pair with two controls from the same rollout: a gap-matched non-revisit pair that measures generic temporal stability and a short-range pair that estimates short-horizon consistency. These comparisons produce \emph{MemoryGain} (MG), the revisit advantage over the temporal baseline, and the \emph{Normalized Memory Ratio} (NMR), which normalizes this advantage by the short-to-baseline dynamic range. R2M-Bench combines 100 reference scenes with three leave-and-return trajectories to form 300 instances and evaluates appearance fidelity, scene and object identity, local geometry, and persistent state. Across seven action-conditioned video world models, Overall NMR correlates with human consistency judgments at Spearman's $ρ=0.547$ (95\% CI $[0.45,0.63]$). Its within-model correlation magnitude with generated motion is $0.072$, compared with $0.207$ for raw revisit similarity, indicating that relative calibration substantially reduces the slow-motion shortcut. DreamX-World-Memo achieves the highest Overall NMR among the evaluated video models. Together, these results support same-rollout relative calibration as a practical way to distinguish revisit-specific consistency from generic temporal stability.

</details>


### [183] [PAWBench: How Far Are We from Probabilistically Aligned World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27345)
*Yuandong Pu,Le Zhuo,Sayak Paul,Gabriel Jorge Menezes,Avram Đorđević,Shiyang Li,Yifan Zhou,Bin Fu,Wenlong Zhang,Junjun He,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jingbo Xing,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出概率对齐（probabilistic alignment）这一分布级准则，用于评估视频生成模型作为世界模型是否不仅生成单个合理轨迹，还能复现相同初始观测和行为下的行为分布。作者引入PAWBench基准和PAWEval评估协议，在50个场景和11个系统中发现没有任何模型能同时匹配参考概率并覆盖有效行为范围，并进一步探究了语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练对预测分布的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型日益被定位为世界模型，但许多物理过程在相同初始条件下可以有多种有效演化方式。当前评估大多只检验单个视频的合理性，无法验证重复生成是否能恢复正确的分布。这种分布层面的要求即概率对齐，但它尚未被形式化和系统测试，因此亟需建立相应准则与基准来衡量现有模型的差距。

Method: 将概率对齐形式化为世界模型的分布级准则；构建PAWBench基准（涵盖50个场景、11个系统）；设计PAWEval评估协议，通过重复视频展开将生成结果转换为关于可能物理行为的经验分布，并与参考概率进行比对；此外测试语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练三种策略能否重塑模型的预测分布。

Result: 在50个场景和11个视频生成系统上，没有任何模型能够同时匹配参考概率并恢复有效行为范围，表明现有系统与概率对齐的世界建模之间存在明显差距。对语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练的测试也未能完全弥合这一鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成器尚未实现概率对齐的世界建模，本文提供的准则、基准和评估协议为未来向概率对齐世界模型的发展奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Recent video generation models are increasingly framed as world models. Many physical processes can unfold in more than one valid way. Therefore, a world model should reproduce not only a plausible trajectory, but also the distribution of possible behaviors under the same initial observation and action. We call this distribution-level requirement probabilistic alignment. However, existing evaluations largely assess individual-video plausibility and do not test whether repeated generations recover the correct distribution. This raises a central question: how far are current video generators from probabilistically aligned world modeling? To answer it, we formalize probabilistic alignment as a distributional criterion for world models and introduce PAWBench, a benchmark for evaluating video generators as stochastic samplers of world dynamics. We further introduce PAWEval, an outcome-level protocol that converts repeated video rollouts into empirical distributions over possible physical behaviors. Across 50 scenarios and eleven current systems, no model consistently matches the reference probabilities while recovering the range of valid behaviors. Having established this gap, we test whether language prompts, initial noise sampling, or model training can reshape the model's predictive distribution. We believe our work can serve as a foundation for future efforts to move towards probabilistically aligned world modeling.

</details>


### [184] [KnockGS:interaction-Grounded Calibrationof Physical Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27365)
*Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Bowen Jing,Jiyuan Cai,Xiaofeng Wang,Hongsen Lei,Weitao Zhou,Dandan Zhang,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KnockGS，一个交互响应的物理3D高斯框架，通过从物体在已知外力下的动力学中提取时间响应特征，来估计其弹性系数和密度尺度，并将估计值冻结回模拟器中，使其可用于未经拟合的新交互测试。在五个材料目标上，该方法在参数恢复和响应级保真度上均优于响应检索、全局回归和固定默认材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理集成3D高斯表示虽然可以对可变形物体进行显式材料模型下的模拟与渲染，但均假设材料参数已知或手动指定，限制了材料参数需要从观测动力学中推断的实际应用场景。

Method: 将力诱导的响应转化为校准信号：从观测动力学中提取时间响应特征，据此估计两个材料尺度（弹性系数和密度），将估计值冻结并写回同一模拟器，以测试其在该模拟器从未拟合过的交互上的泛化能力。

Result: 在五个保留的材料目标上，KnockGS在尺度恢复上显著优于响应检索、全局回归和固定默认材料方法；冻结后的估计在方向和大小不同的交互下仍保持预测性。

Conclusion: 交互响应携带足够的信息来校准物理3D高斯表示中的材料尺度，该工作是迈向渲染外观与模拟响应一致的交互式PhysicalGS系统校准的第一步。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations now allow reconstructed deformable objects to be simulated and rendered under explicit material models. Existing pipelines, however, assume that material parameters are known or manually specified, limiting their applicability when these parameters must be inferred from observed object dynamics. We propose KnockGS, an interaction-response PhysicalGS framework that estimates the elasticity and density scales of a 3D Gaussian object from its dynamics under a known applied force. Rather than treating physical simulation only as a forward process, we turn the force-induced response into a calibration signal: temporal response features are xtracted from the observed dynamics, the two material scales are estimated from those features, and the estimate is then frozen and written back into the same simulator so that it can be tested on an interaction it was never fitted to.We evaluate the framework on both parameter recovery and response-level fidelity. The estimated scales are compared against hidden ground truth, and the re-simulated object is measured against the target using 3D particle trajectories, response-curve statistics, and rendered-frame quality. Across five held-out material targets, our method recovers the scales substantially more accurately than response retrieval, global regression, or a fixed default material, and the frozen estimate remains predictive under interactions that differ in direction and in magnitude. Interaction response therefore carries enough information to calibrate material scales in physically grounded 3D Gaussian representations.Our study is a first step toward interactive PhysicalGS systems that calibrate a Gaussian asset whose rendered appearance and simulated response are consistent.

</details>


### [185] [Successive Capacity Growth: Task-Complexity-Driven Width and Depth Expansion for Vision Transformer Encoders in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27367)
*Frederik Berenz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCG方法，让JEPA世界模型的编码器从极小规模（28.3万参数）开始，通过任务无关的测试-验证机制在宽度或深度上逐步增长，配合Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer防止语义维度坍缩。在三个环境上验证，相比固定大模型实现最高23%的预测损失改善和56倍的参数效率提升，零误报扩展且函数逐位保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的JEPA世界模型采用固定大小的Vision Transformer编码器，存在两个问题：对小任务过度配置（计算浪费），对大任务配置不足（性能受限）；且注意力头之间存在显著冗余。固定预分配最大容量的范式既低效又缺乏自适应性。

Method: 1) 从最小编码器（1头、2层、283K参数）出发；2) 通过宽度扩展（增加注意力头以提升低层语义能力）或深度扩展（增加Transformer块以提升高层语义抽象）逐步增长模型；3) 采用任务无关的测试-验证机制，利用函数保持扩展（function-preserving expansion）安全地试验架构变更，若预测损失未改善则回滚；4) 引入Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer（SIGReg）确保所有语义维度统计独立且与预测目标对齐，防止训练坍缩。

Result: 在60维多目标动力学任务上，SCG自然触发深度扩展，预测损失比固定小基线改善20.3%，参数效率是扩展到固定大模型的56倍；在2D导航任务上，单次宽度扩展即比固定大模型改善23%；在全部三个环境中均达到或超过固定小基线性能，零误报扩展（ratio=1.0，绝对差异=0.0），保证函数逐位保真。

Conclusion: JEPA世界模型编码器无需预先分配最大容量，可按任务需求逐步增长。自适应增长架构在保持表征质量的同时，显著提升了计算和数据效率，为世界模型编码器设计提供了新的替代范式。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling typically employ fixed-size Vision Transformer encoders that are over-provisioned for simple tasks and under-provisioned for complex ones, with significant redundancy across attention heads. We propose Successive Capacity Growth (SCG), a method that starts from a minimal encoder (1 head, 2 layers, 283K parameters) and grows incrementally in width (adding attention heads for low-level semantic capacity) or depth (adding transformer blocks for higher-order semantic abstraction), driven by a task-agnostic test-and-verify mechanism that exploits function-preserving expansion to safely trial architectural changes and roll back if they do not improve prediction loss. The Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) ensures that all learned semantic dimensions remain statistically independent and aligned with the predictive objective, preventing collapse even as the architecture grows. On a 60-dimensional multi-object dynamics task, SCG naturally triggers depth expansion, improving prediction loss by 20.3% over the fixed small baseline with 56 times greater parameter efficiency than scaling to the fixed large model; on a 2D navigation task, a single width expansion yields even an 23% improvement over the fixed large model. Across all three tested environments of increasing complexity, the adaptive encoder matches or exceeds the fixed small baseline, with zero false-positive expansions and bit-exact function preservation (ratio = 1.0, absolute difference = 0.0). The take-away is that JEPA world model encoders need not be pre-allocated at maximum capacity - they can grow successively as the task demands, achieving significant compute and data efficiency while maintaining representation quality.

</details>


### [186] [LeVJEPA: Efficient & Scalable Video Pretraining without the Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27395)
*Lukas Kuhn,Lucas Maes,Giuseppe Serra,Quentin Le Lidec,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeVJEPA，这是首个在LeJEPA无崩塌目标函数下训练的视频编码器，去除了传统方法中的EMA目标编码器、stop-gradient和容量受限预测器，仅用一个编码器和一个投影器即可训练，通过SIGReg正则化在可证明保证下排除表征崩塌。该方法的预训练成本仅由编码器观察的token数量决定，均匀随机token丢弃可大幅降低计算量并提升下游精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频承载物理世界的时间结构，但从视频学习表征的计算成本高昂。现有自监督方法要么依赖架构不对称性（EMA目标编码器、stop-gradient、容量受限预测器）来防止表征崩塌，要么通过像素空间的掩码重建来规避崩塌，两者都带来额外计算开销。作者希望去除这些机制，使视频成为通用视觉预训练的更高效、可行的数据源。

Method: LeVJEPA采用LeJEPA的崩塌无关目标函数，仅由单一编码器和投影器组成。训练目标是对视频片段的全局面和局部面做不变性损失，并由SIGReg正则化提供可证明的崩塌排除保证。该目标仅含单一超参数。由于分支间无对称性要求，编码器可用块因果注意力（block-causal attention）训练，使时间顺序成为编码器自身的属性。同时采用均匀随机token丢弃来控制编码器观察的token数量，从而降低计算成本。

Result: 在相同epoch和相同数据上，LeVJEPA在ViT-S/B/L架构上以5.6到20.8倍更少的预训练计算量匹配或超越V-JEPA 2；在匹配总FLOPs时，在ImageNet-1K上超过最强视频基线7.6个点，同时在运动中心基准上保持竞争力。与计算量匹配的、在相同视频帧上训练的DINOv2相比，LeVJEPA在外观中心评估上接近该图像预训练编码器，同时在运动中心精度上几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一旦去除其计算开销，视频可以成为通用视觉预训练的可行且在多方面更优的数据源。LeVJEPA验证了无需架构不对称性和像素重建的简洁视频表征学习方法，在效率与性能间取得了显著优势。
