A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [253] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在诊断分类法"(Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy)框架，用于构造分类器安全防护层并开发互补诊断方法，以识别分类器自信决策中哪些可以被信任。该框架通过维度优化的嵌入、识别约29%训练样本的潜在支持向量、以及构建诊断分类法来标记不同处理需求的提示。实验发现约77%的自信决策对移除单个词元不稳健，可分为置信度校准失败和可被利用的捷径两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器在部署时经常产生过度自信的错误决策，特别是在安全敏感场景（如提示注入检测）中。需要一个系统性的框架来区分哪些自信决策是可靠的、哪些是有问题的，并提供诊断与补救方案。

Method: 框架包含三个组成部分：(i) 维度优化分类器——通过交叉验证选择嵌入维度而非固定预设；(ii) 利用约29%的训练样本作为潜在支持向量，识别能改变分类器预测标签的关键词元；(iii) 基于这些词元及其攻击强度构建诊断分类法，将提示分为四类：可安全依赖、启发式偏差、启发式覆盖、上下文不足（需人工复核）。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，约77%的自信决策在移除单个词元后不再稳健，且这种脆弱性可区分为两种截然不同的失败模式：置信度校准失败与真正可被利用的捷径。分类法中的每个区域都提供了相应的补救策略。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI安全分类器的部署提供了一套端到端的可操作指南，使系统能够区分可依赖的决策与需要进一步审查的决策，并为每种诊断出的提示类型推荐具体的补救措施。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [254] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准，定义了五个标准化任务以模拟从IID到极端统计异质性的真实工业场景，并系统评估了多种联邦优化算法和神经网络架构，公开代码和数据划分以建立可复现的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的工业预测性维护需要大量run-to-failure数据，但这类数据往往稀缺；联邦学习提供了在不共享传感器数据的情况下协同训练预测模型的范式，然而该领域目前缺乏统一的评估框架，导致各研究结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 基于广泛使用的NASA C-MAPSS数据集构建联邦RUL估计基准FedCMAPSS，设计五个标准化任务来模拟从理想IID到极端统计异质性的真实工业场景，并对多种最新的联邦优化算法在不同神经网络架构上进行系统性评估。

Result: 建立了可复现的基线结果，公开了源代码和数据划分，系统评估了多种联邦优化算法在不同神经架构上的性能表现，揭示了不同异构性场景下算法的适用性。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS为联邦预测性维护研究提供了标准化、可复现的评估基础，填补了该领域缺乏统一比较框架的空白，有助于推动联邦RUL估计方法的开发和公平比较。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [255] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NeoTriFuse，一个可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，用于处理新生儿监护数据中的缺失问题。通过将缺失率建模为显式的可靠性信号，动态调节各模态（静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器、患者级统计摘要）的贡献，同时联合优化死亡率预测和住院时长辅助目标。在F1=0.6736±0.0216、AUROC=0.9454±0.0056上达到有竞争力的性能，消融实验表明局部-全局时间架构和患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡率预测面临极端类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质性、多尺度时间动态和大量数据缺失等挑战。现有多模态方法大多将缺失视为预处理问题，而非将其作为可利用的信息信号，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 提出NeoTriFuse框架：(1) 将缺失性建模为显式的可靠性信号；(2) 集成三类模态——静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器（捕捉多尺度时间动态）、患者级统计摘要；(3) 通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节各模态在融合时的贡献；(4) 联合优化死亡率预测主任务和住院时长辅助任务。

Result: NeoTriFuse取得F1=0.6736±0.0216和AUROC=0.9454±0.0056的竞争性表现。消融实验显示局部-全局时间架构与患者级统计摘要分支对预测性能贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异构观测完整性下对阈值依赖指标提供额外提升。敏感性分析表明性能对超参数设置稳健。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的多模态融合是在现实临床缺失条件下进行新生儿死亡率预测的实用方法，验证了将缺失性作为可靠性信号而非预处理问题的设计理念的有效性。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [256] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SF-GNN框架，通过识别并过滤偏置边（结合敏感同质性和结构传播放大因子），在消息传递过程中随机边缘过滤，从源头缓解GNN的结构性偏见，在五大数据集上实现公平性提升并保持预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知GNN方法主要在全局层面约束表示或预测分布，未显式控制聚合过程中偏见信息传播的局部结构路径，因此需要一种从源头缓解结构偏见的轻量级、架构无关的框架。

Method: SF-GNN首先结合敏感同质性（sensitive homophily）与结构传播放大因子（hub participation和triadic closure）识别易偏置边；然后在每次消息传递步骤中引入随机边缘过滤（stochastic edge filtering），有选择性地降权或移除这些边，同时保留其余图结构；训练过程还加入带预热机制的统计平价正则化器以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，SF-GNN在保持一致预测性能的同时取得了稳定的公平性提升，相比近期公平感知GNN基线实现了更好的公平-准确率权衡。

Conclusion: SF-GNN通过在传播源头（边层面）而非全局层面干预偏见，能够更有效地缓解敏感同质性下GNN的不公平行为，且其轻量、架构无关的特性使其具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [257] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairGIN是一个面向扩张中共享单车系统的公平感知图神经网络，通过扩张模拟增量训练、基于注意力的知识迁移和公平性感知优化三个组件，解决冷启动预测和资源分配公平性问题，在纽约和西雅图数据集上实现了最先进的预测精度并显著降低收入相关的分配差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统持续扩张带来了两个关键挑战：(1) 新部署站点缺乏历史骑行记录，导致基于图的模型在演化网络上训练与推理分布不匹配（冷启动问题）；(2) 历史需求数据可能编码结构性不平等，低收入社区的较低骑行量反映的可能是基础设施接入受限而非潜在需求疲弱，直接基于此类数据训练会强化现有的出行不平等。

Method: FairGIN包含三个核心组件：(1) Expansion-Simulated Increment Training：在训练过程中随机模拟网络扩张，缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer：结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将数据丰富的现有站点表示迁移到数据稀疏的新站点；(3) Fairness-Aware Optimization：引入收入分层正则化与公平校准的部署评分，支持更具包容性的站点布局规划。

Result: 在纽约和西雅图的实验中，FairGIN在多种扩张场景下达到了最先进的预测精度，同时在不牺牲整体系统效率的前提下，显著降低了基于收入的差异。

Conclusion: FairGIN证明了在共享单车系统扩张过程中，可以通过算法设计同时兼顾预测准确性与资源分配的公平性，为低收入社区的移动基础设施布局提供了更包容的决策支持。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [258] [Distributed Training using an Intelligent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26453)
*Nihar Shah,Ben Blier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将网络视为训练中的主动参与者，通过结合组播技术复制出站流量、FPGA内联聚合入站流量，以及基于网络拓扑和这些技术生成丰富同步调度（旋转的集群岛）的优化框架，将数据中心内的分布式训练技术扩展到广域网（WAN），在由九个城市组成的、模拟DoubleZero网络的拓扑上展示了最优调度如何随网络能力变化，从而缩小与同地训练这一金标准的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广域网（WAN）上的分布式训练极具挑战性，因为计算岛的持续参数交换受限于有限的带宽、高延迟和不均匀的拓扑结构。现有训练方案主要在数据中心内进行，缺乏针对WAN特性的协同设计，导致其难以接近同地训练的理想性能。

Method: 在系统层面，利用组播技术复制出站流量以缓解出口瓶颈，并利用内联FPGA聚合入站流量以缓解入口瓶颈——这两种技术已在数据中心内用于跨工作节点训练，本文将其扩展到WAN。在算法层面，开发了一个优化框架，围绕底层网络拓扑和这些技术产生丰富的同步调度方案（即旋转的集群岛），以最大化信息交换。

Result: 在基于DoubleZero网络（一个同时具备组播和FPGA两种技术的实时可编程WAN）建模的九城市拓扑上进行验证，展示了最优调度方案如何随网络能力的变化而转移，证明了所提方法能够缩小与同地训练这一金标准之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过让网络成为分布式训练的主动参与者，并协同设计系统技术与算法调度，可以显著缩小WAN分布式训练与同地训练的差距，为地理分布式大规模模型训练提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Distributed training across a wide area network (WAN) is challenging, as continuous parameter exchange by islands of compute is constrained by limited bandwidth, high latency, and uneven topology. We propose making the network an active participant in training. On the systems side, such networks should leverage (i) multicast technology to replicate outbound traffic and (ii) in-line FPGAs to aggregate inbound traffic, to ease egress and ingress bottlenecks. These technologies are used for training across workers within a data center, but this paper extends them to the WAN. On the algorithms side, we develop an optimization framework that produces rich synchronization schedules (namely, rotating cliques of islands) around the underlying network topology and these technologies, to maximize information exchange. Finally, we illustrate this on a nine-city topology modeled on the DoubleZero network, a live programmable WAN equipped with both technologies, and show how the optimal schedules shift with the network's capabilities. Together, these can narrow the gap to the gold standard of colocated training.

</details>


### [259] [Diff Mining: Logit Differences Reveal Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26462)
*Greg Kocher,Robert West,Clément Dumas,Julian Minder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff Mining框架，通过比较微调模型与基础模型的输出logits差值来识别微调过程中学习到的行为或目标。该框架仅需访问输出logits，不需模型内部结构，通过两个模块化阶段（逐上下文logit差异提取和信号聚合）构建可解释的token指纹。实验证明其在微调域检测、偏见识别等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为语言模型改进和引入新行为的黄金标准，但微调过程中具体涌现了哪些行为往往不明确。随着模型能力增强，理解微调过程愈发重要，因为微调期间可能产生不需要的行为。现有模型差异比较方法多需要访问模型内部结构，限制了其可扩展性和实用性。

Method: Diff Mining框架包含两个模块化阶段：(1) 在参考语料库上提取微调模型与基础模型之间逐上下文的logit差异；(2) 聚合这些差异信号以构建代表微调目标的可解释token集合。聚合方法探索了两种：简单的Top-K频率方法和基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）的方法，后者可将多个微调目标分解为不同的token簇。

Result: 在微调域检测任务上，Diff Mining显著优于最先进的模型差异比较方法，在识别相关token和下游性能（当可解释性代理获得提取的token集合时）方面均表现更好；在注入偏见的模型中，无需针对性地探测即可识别出超过三分之一的偏见。

Conclusion: Diff Mining框架在开发检测微调目标的审计工具方面展现出潜力，为理解微调模型学习到的行为提供了一种仅依赖输出logits的轻量级、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Finetuning has become the gold standard for refining existing behaviors and inducing new ones in language models, yet it often remains unclear exactly which behaviors emerge during this process. As models grow ever more capable, understanding finetuning better becomes increasingly important, particularly since unwanted behaviors may arise during finetuning. In this paper, we introduce Diff Mining, a simple yet effective framework for identifying what a finetuned model has learned by comparing its logits to those of its base model. Diff Mining effectively surfaces salient tokens that are amplified in the finetuned model, serving as a fingerprint of its training -- even on text unrelated to the finetuning domain. Unlike many existing model diffing methods which require model internals, Diff Mining only needs access to output logits and scales to large models. The framework consists of two modular stages: (i) extracting per-context logit differences between the finetuned and base models on a reference corpus, and (ii) aggregating the resulting signals to construct an interpretable token set representing the finetune. For aggregation, we explore both a simple Top-K frequency method and a Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF)-based approach for disentangling multiple finetuning objectives into distinct token clusters. Empirically, Diff Mining succeeds across diverse settings: on finetune domain detection, it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art model diffing methods both in identifying relevant tokens and in downstream performance when an interpretability agent is given access to the extracted token set; on models with injected biases, it identifies more than one third of the biases without targeted probing. Overall, our framework shows promise in developing auditing tools to detect finetuning objectives.

</details>


### [260] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH是一个课程学习框架，通过显式建模强化学习环境中由先决关系诱导的有向无环课程图(DAG)，进行主动学习。它首先通过采样多样化的课程路径扩展覆盖范围，然后将训练重新分配到尚未掌握的区域。实验表明PATH在多种环境中展现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习环境中，环境之间由先决关系（如难度递增的编辑或参数增量）连接，形成有向无环课程图(DAG)。这种结构通常仅被隐式利用，但显式建模该结构有助于提升训练效果。

Method: PATH框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 扩展覆盖——通过采样多样化的课程路径扩大学习覆盖范围；(2) 重新分配——将训练资源重新分配至尚未掌握的区域。

Result: 在多种多样化环境中的实验表明，PATH显式利用课程图结构，取得了强的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模环境间的先决关系图并进行主动课程学习，能够有效提升强化学习训练的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [261] [Shared Actors Need Not Share Critics: Effects of Value Mismatch in Parallel Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26481)
*Zhenya Liu,Yang Meng,Zhuokai Zhao,Xuefeng Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究并行强化学习中共享 critic 在不同环境间的价值不匹配问题。当同一任务的不同环境（如程序化生成关卡、随机动力学）对相同观测赋予不同期望回报时，共享 critic 被迫调和不同价值目标，导致优势估计系统偏移。理论分析（基于多环境 bandit 模型）揭示了这种偏移如何使策略更新偏向无用动作、削弱甚至反转有用动作。作者提出最小干预：仅将环境索引作为额外条件输入给 critic（多头条件 critic），使其分离价值目标。实验在 CartPole、MuJoCo、BipedalWalker 和 Procgen 上验证了理论预测，并在 16 个 Procgen 游戏中使未见关卡的平均归一化回报提升 40.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行强化学习（如程序化生成关卡、随机动力学、课程学习）中常用单一 critic 覆盖所有采样环境，但不同环境可能对 critic 可见的相同输入赋予不同的期望回报。共享 critic 被迫在多个相冲突的价值目标间调和，系统性地偏移单个环境内的优势估计，这可能损害学习动态。现有研究多关注标量估计器方差，而未识别价值不匹配作为影响随机学习动态的直接机制。

Method: 1) 理论部分：构建含多个环境和公共最优臂的 bandit 模型，刻画价值不匹配如何重新分配策略更新，并对比三种 oracle 过程（无基线、共享价值、环境特有价值）的平均 logit 更新与收敛路径。2) 干预方案：仅向 critic 提供一个记录的（logged）环境索引，使其能分离各环境的价值目标（multihead conditional critic）。3) 实验验证：在受控的 CartPole、MuJoCo 以及更复杂的 BipedalWalker、Procgen 环境中验证理论预测。

Result: 理论结果表明：三种 oracle 在固定策略下具有相同的平均 logit 更新并收敛到相同最优策略，但实现的学习路径差异显著；共享 critic 的价值不匹配会强化无益动作并衰减甚至反转有用动作的更新。受控实验验证了预测的价值偏移、优势偏移和性能差距。在 BipedalWalker 和 Procgen 中，环境索引条件干预带来更稳定的学习和更高回报。在全部 16 个 Procgen 游戏中，多头条件 critic 在每游戏 600 个未见关卡上的平均归一化回报提升 40.8%。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明价值不匹配是 critic 共享导致随机学习动态退化的直接机制，该效应不能被标量估计器方差单独解释。实验表明，仅以环境索引为条件（而非需要环境信息）就能使 critic 分离价值目标，是一种在并行强化学习中广泛有效的最小干预方案。

Abstract: When a single policy is trained in parallel across multiple environments of the same task, such as procedurally generated levels, randomized dynamics, or curricula, implementations commonly use one critic across all sampled environments. Yet different environments can assign different expected returns to the same input visible to the critic. A critic without environment information must then reconcile distinct value targets, systematically shifting the sampled advantages within individual environments. Using illustrative bandit models with multiple environments and a common optimal arm, we characterize how this value mismatch redistributes sampled policy updates, reinforcing unhelpful actions while attenuating or even reversing useful ones. The oracle processes using no baseline, the shared value, or the value specific to the sampled environment have the same mean logit update at a fixed policy and converge to the same optimal policy, yet their realized learning paths can differ sharply. The analysis motivates a minimal intervention: give only a logged environment index to the critic so that it can separate the value targets. Controlled CartPole and MuJoCo experiments expose the predicted shifted values, advantages, and performance gaps. In the more complex BipedalWalker and Procgen settings, the same intervention yields more stable learning and higher returns. Across all $16$ Procgen games, the multihead conditional critic improves aggregate normalized return on $600$ unseen levels per game by $40.8\%$. In conclusion, the theory identifies value mismatch as a direct mechanism through which critic sharing can degrade stochastic learning dynamics, not captured by scalar estimator variance alone, and the experiments show that conditioning on an index is broadly effective in parallel reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [262] [A Unified Framework for Fair and Personalized Decentralized Learning under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26493)
*Krishnendu S. Tharakan,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DMFL-SQ算法，统一了图基个性化、不可知公平性和压缩事件触发通信三个维度，在有限通信约束下实现去中心化学习中的公平训练。该算法融合稀疏化、量化和事件触发同步来降低通信开销，同时在小批量平方Moreau包络平稳性下达到O(T^{-1/2})的非凸收敛率，并提供了PAC-Bayes泛化保证。在CIFAR-10和MUSMET EEG数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习虽然改善了可扩展性、隐私和鲁棒性，但加剧了三大根本性挑战：跨客户端统计异质性、客户端级公平性和严苛的通信约束。作者提出核心问题：在有限通信下，去中心化学习能实现多公平？现有研究往往单独处理这些挑战，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出了DMFL-SQ算法，一种去中心化多任务学习算法：1) 在通信图上耦合个性化模型训练；2) 引入不可知混合公平目标函数；3) 通过稀疏化、量化和事件触发同步三种通信压缩技术降低通信开销。理论分析涵盖非凸目标下的收敛保证和公平感知混合目标的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: DMFL-SQ在稀疏、量化、事件触发通信条件下，对一般非凸目标在期望平方Moreau包络平稳性上达到O(T^{-1/2})收敛率，并推导出公平感知混合目标的PAC-Bayes泛化保证。CIFAR-10和MUSMET EEG数据集实验表明，该方法大幅降低通信量，同时保持预测性能并提升客户端间公平性。

Conclusion: 理论与实验结果共同表明，在去中心化学习中，个性化、公平性和通信效率可以同时实现，且不牺牲主导收敛率。这回答了"有限通信下公平性可达多高"这一开放问题。

Abstract: Decentralized learning systems aim to collaboratively train models across multiple clients without relying on a central coordinator. While decentralization improves scalability, privacy, and robustness, it also exacerbates three fundamental challenges: statistical heterogeneity across clients, fairness in client-level performance, and stringent communication constraints. This raises a natural question: \emph{how fair can decentralized learning be under limited communication?} We address this question by presenting a unified framework for decentralized learning under communication constraints, bringing together graph-based personalization, agnostic fairness, and compressed event-triggered communication. Specifically, we propose a new algorithm DMFL-SQ, a decentralized multi-task learning algorithm that couples personalized model training over a communication graph with an agnostic mixture fairness objective, while reducing communication through sparsification, quantization, and event-triggered synchronization. We establish convergence guarantees for general non-convex objectives and show that DMFL-SQ achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate in expected squared Moreau-envelope stationarity despite sparse, quantized, and event-triggered communication. We further derive PAC-Bayes generalization guarantees for the fairness-aware mixture objective. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and the real heterogeneous MUSMET EEG dataset demonstrate that DMFL-SQ substantially reduces communication while maintaining predictive performance and improving fairness across clients. Together, our theoretical and empirical results show that personalization, fairness, and communication efficiency can be jointly achieved in decentralized learning while preserving the dominant convergence rate.

</details>


### [263] [A Single Suffix to Break Them All: Basin-Aware Jailbreaks for Merged Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26506)
*Yu Zhe,Yixin Tan,Junhao Wei,Wang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模型合并中一个被忽视的越狱风险：即使所有构成模型都经过个体安全对齐，合并后的模型仍可能泄露预训练基础模型固有的漏洞。作者提出Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)方法，通过最小-最大优化在合并空间中生成可跨合并模型家族迁移的对抗后缀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要将模型合并的安全风险归因于不安全的构成模型，隐含假设合并个体对齐的模型能保持安全性。作者发现这一假设不成立——模型合并会揭示预训练基础模型中固有的越狱风险，即使所有构成模型均个体安全对齐。这构成了一个被先前工作忽视的新威胁场景。

Method: 作者提出Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)方法，将越狱攻击后缀的生成形式化为合并空间上的最小-最大优化问题。攻击者在无需访问精确合并系数或构成模型检查点的情况下，构造能够在共享同一预训练骨干的合并模型家族间泛化的对抗后缀。

Result: 在多种基础模型架构和合并设置下的实验表明，BAJ方法实现了持续高水平的迁移成功率，并且对现有防御手段仍然有效。

Conclusion: 模型合并这一高效技术存在此前未被认识的安全隐患——合并不应被视为"安全叠加"的过程。该工作揭示了一种源自预训练基础模型的新型越狱风险，并展示了即使所有构成模型均安全对齐，合并操作仍可能引入可利用的安全漏洞，对现有合并安全假设提出了重要挑战。

Abstract: Model merging enables combining multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Prior work primarily attributes merging risks to unsafe constituent models, implicitly assuming that merging individually aligned models preserves safety. In contrast, we show that model merging reveals a previously overlooked jailbreak risk rooted in the pretrained foundation model, even when all constituent models are individually safety-aligned. Motivated by this observation, we study a new threat setting where an attacker constructs jailbreak prompts that generalize across merged models sharing the same pretrained backbone, without access to the exact merging coefficients or constituent checkpoints. To exploit this phenomenon, we propose \textbf{Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)}, which formulates jailbreak generation as a min--max optimization over the merging space to produce transferable adversarial suffixes across merged model families. Experiments across diverse backbones and merging settings show that BAJ achieves consistently high transfer success rates and remains effective under existing defenses.

</details>


### [264] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了宽随机神经网络混合输入导数的概率上界。作者证明，对于Xavier初始化的tanh网络，当网络宽度满足特定下界时，高阶混合导数的上界为 C_0 |u|! (C_1 L)^{|u|-1} ∏_{j∈u} β_j，其中一阶导数界与深度无关，而平方自由混合导数随深度呈多项式增长（L^{|u|-1}）。该结果还导出了网络实现Euclidean Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界，后者与拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）积分及QMC训练分析相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络的高阶导数性质对其训练与泛化性能至关重要。现有基于Euclidean算子范数的确定性分析方法给出的导数上界通常随深度指数增长，这在深层网络中过于悲观。作者希望为足够宽的Gaussian网络建立更紧的导数界，以理解网络正则性如何随深度演化，并建立与QMC积分理论之间的桥梁。

Method: 作者采用概率分析方法取代确定性算子范数估计。关键技巧是：将最高阶导数中线性项分离出来，并通过可测有限网（measurable finite nets）控制对应的切向方向。结合激活函数导数满足阶乘增长界（tanh天然满足）的假设以及Xavier初始化的Gaussian权重重尾性质，通过高概率集中不等式推导出导数的统一上界。

Result: 对于标量输出tanh网络（Gaussian权重+Xavier初始化），在宽度满足 n ≥ C(L^3 n_0^2(1+log n_0) + L^2(1+log(L/η))) 时，以概率≥1-η对所有非空u⊆[n_0]和x∈[0,1]^{n_0}成立 |D^u R_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)| ≤ C_0 |u|! (C_1 L)^{|u|-1} ∏_{j∈u} β_j(η,n_0)。一阶导数界与深度L无关，|u|阶平方自由混合导数至多以L^{|u|-1}多项式增长。由此导出网络Euclidean Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在足够宽的随机tanh网络中，导数上界从指数增长改善为多项式增长，这为深层网络的正则性提供了更乐观的理论刻画。所得Sobolev范数界为将网络正则性纳入准蒙特卡洛（QMC）积分及QMC-based训练的分析提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [265] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"可量化性"（quantifiability）作为药物筛选中的独立分诊目标，即药物在随访剂量-反应实验中能否产生可用的效能估计，而非仅仅关注生物活性是否确认。研究发现，可量化性可从前期的低成本筛选中高精度预测，预测信息主要来自筛选特征而非分子结构，且模型可泛化到新化学骨架和未见的测定机制家族。量化概率随反应幅度和测定背景显著变化，表明可量化性感知的分诊策略能更好地配置昂贵的剂量-反应分析资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高通量筛选策略主要根据生物活性确认与否来优先排序化合物进入昂贵的剂量-反应实验，隐含假设"确认的活性必然产生可量化的效能"。然而，实际中活性化合物仍可能无法产生可报告的剂量-反应估计。因此需要将"可量化性"作为与生物活性不同的独立分诊目标进行建模，以更合理地分配稀缺的随访测试资源。

Method: 构建了一个建模框架，专门预测随访测试能否产生可用的效能估计（可量化性）。该框架将其作为与生物活性不同的独立分诊目标处理，利用前期的低成本筛选数据作为预测特征，评估筛选特征与分子结构各自对预测的贡献，并测试模型向新化学骨架和未见过测定机制家族的泛化能力。

Result: 可量化性从前期低成本筛选中具有强可预测性，且大多数预测信息来自观测到的筛选特征而非分子结构。基于响应的预测因子在面对新化学骨架时保持稳健，并能泛化到留出的测定机制家族。同时，成功量化的概率随反应幅度和测定背景的变化显著而强烈。

Conclusion: 实验可测性（区别于生物活性）是被筛选结果的可预测属性。可量化性感知的分诊策略能改善昂贵的剂量-反应分析能力的配置，为高通量筛选流程提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [266] [Chart2SVG: Editable SVG Generation from Raster Chart Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26544)
*Jinning Cui,Lu Chen,Haoyan Shi,Yue He,Chenglong Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chart2SVG，一个将静态栅格图表转换为结构化、语义丰富的SVG格式的多模态大语言模型，支持程序化编辑和下游可视化操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态栅格图表缺乏结构信息，无法进行程序化编辑和交互操作，限制了智能可视化工具的发展。现有方法要么生成视觉上相似但不含语义结构的SVG，要么缺乏对图表几何图元及其功能角色的联合理解。

Method: 将图表特定的语义token引入视觉-语言模型中，同时捕获几何图元和功能角色；构建Beagle+数据集（33K规范化和结构蒸馏的图表样本）；采用专门训练目标结合渲染感知的后训练阶段，生成视觉准确且结构一致的SVG；构建Chart Structure Graph (CSG)以暴露视觉依赖关系，支持交互探索、图表再利用和布局复用。

Result: 实验表明Chart2SVG在重建保真度和下游编辑实用性方面显著优于基线方法，证明了其在智能交互式可视化工具中的潜力。

Conclusion: Chart2SVG推动了大语言模型在图表结构化恢复中的应用，为构建智能化和交互式可视化工具奠定了基础，支持更高级别的图表操作任务。

Abstract: We present Chart2SVG, a multimodal large language model that converts static raster charts into structurally organized, semantically enriched SVGs that support programmatic editing. By incorporating chart-specific semantic tokens into a vision-language model, Chart2SVG captures both geometric primitives and their functional roles. To support robust structural recovery, we introduce Beagle+, a dataset of 33K canonicalized and structurally distilled chart samples. Our approach combines specialized training objectives with a rendering-aware post-training phase, producing SVGs that are both visually accurate and structurally consistent. To facilitate higher-level manipulations, we construct a Chart Structure Graph (CSG) that exposes visual dependencies, enabling tasks such as interactive exploration, chart repurposing, and layout reuse. Experiments show that Chart2SVG substantially outperforms baselines in reconstruction fidelity and downstream editing utility, advancing the development of intelligent and interactive visualization tools.

</details>


### [267] [Arrive and Survive: Scaling Safe Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning from One-Bit Failure Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26571)
*Guopeng Li,Yiyang Duan,Yiru Jiao,Chengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe-CRL方法，针对失败终止型马尔可夫决策过程中对比强化学习（CRL）存在的估计偏差问题，通过质量加权InfoNCE和对数生存质量得分两项修正，仅利用失败终止的一比特信号实现安全的目标条件策略学习，在12个机器人导航和运动任务中显著提升了生存率与目标达成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在失败终止的MDP中，现有CRL仅在构造正样本时考虑失败前的未来目标，忽略失败终止所移除的概率质量。理论分析表明这会引发对目标达成价值的系统性高估偏差，导致近失败轨迹提供过强监督，强化不安全动作，最终造成策略学习失败和不可持续的目标达成行为。

Method: 提出两项最小但有效的修正：(1) 质量加权InfoNCE（mass-weighted InfoNCE），校正评论家学习中短存活未来被过度加权的问题；(2) 对数生存质量得分（log-survival-mass score），恢复策略优化中缺失的生存概率质量。该方法命名为Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL)，仅需失败终止提供的一比特信号。

Result: 在12个易失败的机器人导航和运动控制任务中，Safe-CRL一致性地提升了生存率，并在目标达成性能上显著优于Scaling-CRL基线。此外，深层Safe-CRL策略展现出复杂的避障行为。

Conclusion: 本文补全了失败终止条件下的CRL理论，并提供了一个可扩展的安全强化学习框架，同时开源了代码。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) scales effectively in goal-conditioned tasks by casting policy learning into a self-supervised contrastive objective. However, in a failure-terminated Markov decision process, established CRL considers pre-failure future goals only when constructing positive samples, without accounting for the probability mass removed by failure termination. Our theoretical analysis shows that this omission induces a systematic overestimation bias in goal-reaching values. Consequently, near-failure trajectories provide disproportionately strong supervision of success despite retaining little future occupancy. Unsafe actions can thereby be reinforced through catastrophic failure bootstrapping, leading to failed policy learning and unsustainable goal-reaching behaviours. To address this problem, we introduce two minimal yet strong corrections: mass-weighted InfoNCE corrects the overweighting of short surviving futures in critic learning, and a log-survival-mass score restores the missing survival mass in policy optimization. The resulting method, Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL), requires only the one-bit signal provided by failure termination to scale safe goal-conditioned policy learning. Across twelve failure-prone robot navigation and locomotion tasks, Safe-CRL consistently improves survival and substantially outperforms the Scaling-CRL baseline in goal-reaching performance. Additionally, deep Safe-CRL policies exhibit complex failure-avoidance behaviours. This study completes the CRL theory under failure termination and provides a scalable safe RL framework. The code is available via https://github.com/RomainLITUD/safe-crl.

</details>


### [268] [Activation Outliers Matter: Robust Recovery for Quantized Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581)
*Tanzila Rahman,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Zhuang Ma,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Yao Wang,Xing Huang,Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Yonghan Dong,Wei Guo,Yaoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的低比特量化方案（MXFP8、MXFP4、HiF4），发现激活量化是4比特量化性能损失的主要来源，并提出残差回退量化（RFQ）框架，通过辅助量化残差路径补偿激活量化误差，在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上显著缩小与BF16基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化是降低MLLMs计算和内存开销的有效途径，但激进的4比特量化会导致显著性能退化。研究者希望通过系统研究识别性能损失的主导因素，并提出有效的补偿策略以实现鲁棒的4比特部署。

Method: 通过大规模消融实验定位性能瓶颈为激活量化而非权重量化；基于此提出残差回退量化（RFQ），该框架在超低比特激活表示基础上增加辅助量化残差路径，显式建模并补偿量化误差，无需架构修改且计算开销可忽略。

Result: MXFP8实现近无损性能，而4比特量化（MXFP4、HiF4）导致显著退化；RFQ在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上持续恢复大部分量化损失的性能，显著缩小与BF16基线的差距，覆盖视频生成和推理两类任务。

Conclusion: 激活量化是超低比特MLLMs性能退化的主导瓶颈，基于残差的激活重建是有效且实用的4比特部署策略，为低比特量化MLLMs提供了重要的优化方向。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [269] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: J-Zero是一个统一的Challenger–Solver–Judge协同进化框架，通过对抗性交互和基于已知排序的偏好对的Judge协同适应，在可验证和不可验证两个领域均实现自我改进，平均超越基线4.2分（可验证）和8.0分（不可验证），并能持续改善至少十轮迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型是通往超级智能的有前景路径，可降低人工监督成本。尽管在可验证领域取得了显著进展，但不可验证领域中的自我进化研究相对缺乏，需要统一框架来同时支持两类领域的自我改进。

Method: 提出J-Zero框架，包含三个组件：Challenger生成难度递增的任务，Solver学习产生更高质量的回应，两者通过对抗性交互协同进化。Judge使用其排序在响应产生前已知的偏好对（即Solver的回答优于Challenger的回答，以及分解重组后的回答优于一次性回答）进行协同适应，而非基于Judge自身的评分。

Result: J-Zero在可验证领域平均超越基线4.2分，在不可验证领域平均超越8.0分；能够持续改进至少十轮迭代，而基线方法在两轮后出现性能退化。

Conclusion: 通过设计不依赖Judge自身评分的已知排序偏好对，J-Zero实现了跨可验证与不可验证领域的统一自我进化框架，避免了奖励黑客与评分偏差问题，展现出更强的鲁棒性和可持续进化能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [270] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SimCast-S2S，一种基于生成式潜空间扩散模型（latent-diffusion）的概率性次季节到季节（S2S）降水预报框架。它通过变分自编码器（VAE）在紧凑潜空间中生成大集合样本，利用LoRA迁移学习在气候模拟数据上预训练后在再分析数据上微调，在再分析数据上超越深度学习基线，且无需后处理即可匹敌甚至超越ECMWF-S2S等业务系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S降水预报具有重大金融和社会影响，但面临弱预测信号、高不确定性以及业务系统计算成本高昂（限制模拟保真度）等挑战。此外，S2S预测需要不确定性量化而非仅有确定性点预报，现有数据驱动方法在概率生成、大规模集合计算效率和训练数据不足三方面存在瓶颈。

Method: SimCast-S2S采用三阶段策略：(1) 使用基于扩散的生成式流水线，从条件分布中有效采样，实现概率预报；(2) 通过变分自编码器（VAE）学习紧凑潜空间，在潜空间中高效生成大规模概率集合，避免物理空间的高计算成本；(3) 采用低秩自适应（LoRA）迁移学习，先在气候模拟大集合上预训练，再在有限的再分析数据上微调，克服扩散模型对大规模训练数据的需求。

Result: 在再分析数据上，SimCast-S2S优于卷积神经网络和U-Net等深度学习基线。尽管仅使用部分大气输入变量且无后处理、偏差校正或校准，SimCast-S2S仍与ECMWF-S2S等先进业务系统竞争相当，并在许多情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 潜空间生成建模结合模拟到再分析的迁移学习，为数据驱动的概率性S2S降水预报提供了一条高效且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [271] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习方法在互补可再生能源场景下进行了全面基准测试，涵盖大型WEC数据集、16 WEC数据集及Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA数据。结果表明Extra Trees在结构化WEC布局数据上表现最佳（相对MLP基线MAE降低63.7%）；STGCN通过显式学习时空交互达到MAE≈167.0 kW、R=0.93；RF BiLSTM混合模型综合预测精度最高（MAE=150.5 kW），相对LSTM降低约75%、相对STGCN提升约10%。实验证明不存在普适最优的单一AI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源领域数据具有多样化特征（结构化布局数据、图结构时空数据、表格时序数据），但缺乏对各类AI架构在多种互补可再生场景下的系统比较。作者旨在提供一个全面的基准测试，厘清不同数据特征下各AI方法的适用边界，为从业者提供模型选型指导，并验证是否存在通用最优架构。

Method: 在三个数据集（大型WEC、16 WEC、Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA）上系统评估了传统ML、集成学习、深度神经网络、RNN、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习方法。针对结构化WEC布局数据，探索随机划分与提升类树集成；针对风电场时序数据，引入STGCN以显式建模空间-时间交互；提出RF BiLSTM混合架构，将随机森林的特征提取能力与双向LSTM的时序建模能力相结合。

Result: （1）在结构化WEC数据上，树集成明显优于传统ML和神经预测器，Extra Trees最优，相对MLP基线MAE降低约63.7%；（2）STGCN在风场预测中达到MAE≈167.0 kW、R=0.93；（3）RF BiLSTM混合模型取得最佳整体精度，MAE=150.5 kW，相对LSTM降低约75%，相对STGCN改进约10%；（4）实验证实不同数据特征对应不同最优架构，无普适最优模型。

Conclusion: 不存在放之四海而皆准的单一AI架构：随机化与提升类集成适合结构化WEC代理建模，图网络在显式空间交互占主导时更具优势，集成循环混合模型在非线性表格关系与时序动态并存时提供最佳平衡。该基准为可再生能源场景中的AI模型选型提供了依据。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [272] [Robust Neural Stimulation Response Modeling Through Meta-Learning and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26649)
*Matthew J Bryan,Daniel C Muir,Felix Schwock,Azadeh Yazdan-Shahmorad,Rajesh P N Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明元学习（MAML）和预训练可应用于神经刺激响应建模，通过在40个非人灵长类光遗传刺激会话上的验证，显著降低了灾难性预测失败（R²<0.05的会话从16/40降至1/40），并将校准需求减少50-90%，为模型驱动的闭环神经刺激在临床中的部署提供了关键支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动的闭环神经刺激在帕金森病、感觉恢复等治疗应用中具有巨大潜力，但其部署受两大障碍制约：1）预测刺激后果的模型在相当比例的会话中灾难性失败；2）逐会话的校准需求与临床时间约束不兼容。本文旨在解决这两个根本性问题，推动模型驱动刺激控制器的临床落地。

Method: 以时间基函数模型（TBFMs）为基础模型来预测状态依赖的神经刺激响应，提出了一种基于模型无关元学习（MAML）的新型架构和算法，实现跨会话预训练。在两只非人灵长类初级感觉运动皮层的40个光遗传刺激会话上进行系统评估。

Result: 元学习大幅降低灾难性预测失败：在1000个校准数据点条件下，测试R²<0.05的会话从单会话训练的16/40降至MAML预训练的1/40；预测区间显著收窄（p<0.05）；在匹配精度下，校准需求降低50-90%，使原本受限于临床会话时间约束的实验变得可行。

Conclusion: 刺激反应中的跨会话结构具有足够的一致性以支持预训练，提供了元学习方法可用于神经刺激的首个实证证据。这为组装标准化多中心刺激数据集以及进一步探索基于元学习的鲁棒闭环刺激提供了动机，直接回应了模型驱动刺激控制器部署中的已知障碍。

Abstract: Objective: Model-based closed-loop neural stimulation holds promise for therapeutic applications ranging from Parkinson's disease to sensory restoration, but deployment has been limited by two obstacles: 1) forecasting models for predicting the consequences of stimulation fail catastrophically on a meaningful fraction of sessions, and 2) per-session calibration requirements are often incompatible with clinical constraints. We address both by demonstrating, for the first time, that meta-learning and pretraining can be applied to neural stimulation response modeling. Methods: Temporal basis function models (TBFMs) forecast state-dependent neural responses to stimulation. We extend TBFMs with cross-session pretraining using a novel architecture and algorithm based on model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML), evaluating them on 40 sessions of optogenetic stimulation in primary sensorimotor cortex of two non-human primates. Results: Meta-learning substantially reduces catastrophic forecast failure: for a 1k calibration set size, sessions with test R-squared < 0.05 drop from 16 of 40 (single-session training) to 1 (MAML-pretrained), and prediction intervals become significantly narrower (p < 0.05). Calibration requirements are reduced by 50-90% at matched accuracy, enabling experiments otherwise infeasible within clinical session-time constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate that cross-session structure in stimulation responses is consistent enough to support pretraining, providing the first empirical evidence that meta-learning approaches are viable for neural stimulation. Significance: The robustness and sample efficiency gains directly address known obstacles to deploying model-based stimulation controllers. Our results motivate community efforts to assemble standardized multi-site stimulation datasets and to further explore meta-learning for robust closed-loop stimulation.

</details>


### [273] [When Privacy Hurts Mergeability: Geometry-Aware Model Merging under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26655)
*Jin Liu,Junkang Liu,Ning Xi,Yinbin Miao,Dawei Wei,Ke Cheng,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究差分隐私（DP）下的模型合并问题，识别了阻碍私有任务模型合并的两个几何障碍——局部尖锐性（local sharpness）和参考漂移（reference drift），并提出DP-Merging框架，通过DP兼容的锐度感知目标和基于参考的对齐正则化器来改善私有模型的合并性。理论推导证明了降低局部曲率和参考漂移可收紧合并损失上界，实验验证了该方法在视觉和语言任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并能从独立微调的任务模型中构建多任务模型而不需要原始数据，但释放的任务模型可能泄漏私有微调数据。差分隐私提供了限制泄漏的原则性机制，然而DP对模型合并的影响尚未被充分理解，私有任务模型难以合并的问题需要系统性研究。

Method: 本文首先从几何角度分析了DP模型合并中的两大障碍：局部尖锐性（任务损失对合并引起的参数位移敏感）和参考漂移（私有任务模型偏离共享预训练初始化的程度放大跨任务干扰）。基于此提出DP-Merging框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) DP兼容的锐度感知目标，引导每个私有任务模型趋向更平坦的损失区域；(2) 基于参考的对齐正则化器，使任务模型保持接近共享预训练初始化。此外推导了合并差距上界，证明降低局部曲率和参考漂移可收紧合并引起的损失增加上界。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务、多个隐私预算下的实验表明，DP-Merging持续提升了私有合并模型的性能，同时保持了底层DP微调过程的隐私保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了DP模型合并的几何特性，提出的DP-Merging框架有效解决了私有任务模型的合并难题，在保障隐私的同时提升了多任务合并性能，为隐私保护下的模型合并提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Abstract: Model merging promises to construct a single multi-task model from independently fine-tuned task models without accessing the original task data. This makes it attractive when task data cannot be centralized, but released task models may still leak private fine-tuning data. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled mechanism for limiting such leakage, yet its effect on model merging remains poorly understood. In this paper, we study the geometry of differentially private model merging and identify two geometric obstacles that make private task models difficult to merge: \emph{local sharpness}, which makes task losses sensitive to the parameter displacement induced by merging, and \emph{reference drift}, which measures the displacement of private task models from the shared pretrained initialization and amplifies cross-task interference. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{DP-Merging}, a geometry-aware framework that improves the mergeability of differentially private task models. DP-Merging uses a DP-compatible sharpness-aware objective to guide each private task model toward flatter loss regions, and a reference-based alignment regularizer to keep task models close to the shared pretrained initialization. We derive a merge-gap upper bound showing that reducing local curvature and reference drift tightens the bound on the loss increase induced by merging. Experiments on vision and language tasks across multiple privacy budgets show that DP-Merging consistently improves private merged-model performance while preserving the privacy guarantees of the underlying DP fine-tuning procedures.

</details>


### [274] [Simple Actors and Deep Critics for Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26659)
*Guhyeon Kang,Jaehwi Lee,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC（轻量Actor、深度Critic）框架，在离线强化学习中颠覆性地将模型容量从生成式actor（如扩散/流匹配策略）转向深度critic，通过残差MLP骨干、n步bootstrap目标和分类交叉熵损失三种技术解决深度critic的三大失效模式，在OGBench上与最强扩散/流匹配基线性能持平，同时推理延迟降低至4倍，无需蒸馏即可媲美一步蒸馏策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习的先进方法依赖生成式actor（扩散、流匹配策略）以捕捉多模态行为，但这些actor在部署时每次决策都需要多次去噪或积分步骤，导致巨大推理开销。由于critic仅在训练阶段使用、部署时被丢弃，而actor在每个决策步骤都运行，因此将容量分配给critic而非actor更有利于推理效率。然而现有MLP critic在离线RL中加深时会引入多种训练不稳定性，实践中critic通常保持浅层，这限制了这种容量分配策略的实现。

Method: 论文首先识别出深度critic在离线RL中加深时的三大失效模式：优化不稳定、bootstrap噪声放大和值域漂移。针对每个问题提出对应解决方案：(1) 使用残差MLP骨干网络解决优化不稳定；(2) 使用n步bootstrap目标缓解bootstrap噪声放大；(3) 采用分类交叉熵损失处理值域漂移。将这些组件与轻量确定性actor结合，构成LAC框架。

Result: 在OGBench基准上，LAC与最强的扩散和流匹配基线性能相当，同时推理延迟降低多达4倍，接近一步蒸馏策略的延迟且无需蒸馏过程。此外，其critic配方可以跨actor参数化迁移，表明该critic设计具有通用性。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是：在离线actor-critic方法中，与其将容量投入复杂的生成式actor，不如将其分配给critic。通过针对性解决深度critic的失效模式，LAC实现了以更高效推理换取同等性能的目标，为离线RL中的容量分配提供了新的设计视角。

Abstract: Recent progress in offline reinforcement learning (RL) has been driven by expressive generative actors such as diffusion and flow-matching policies, which capture multimodal behavior in offline datasets. However, these actors require multiple denoising or integration steps per action and thus incur substantial overhead at every decision in deployment. In this work, we revisit where capacity should be invested in an offline actor--critic method. Since the critic is used only during training and is discarded at deployment while the actor runs at every decision step, allocating capacity to the critic rather than the actor is more favorable for inference-time efficiency. However, scaling MLP critics in offline RL is known to introduce several distinct instabilities that have, in practice, kept critics shallow. We identify three distinct failure modes that arise when critics are deepened in offline RL---optimization, bootstrap-noise amplification, and value-range drift---and address each with a corresponding ingredient: a residual MLP backbone, n-step bootstrap targets, and a categorical cross-entropy loss. Combining these ingredients with a lightweight deterministic actor, we propose LAC (Light Actor, deep Critic). On OGBench, LAC matches the strongest diffusion- and flow-matching baselines while achieving up to 4x lower inference latency, comparable to one-step distilled policies without distillation. Its critic recipe also transfers across actor parametrizations.

</details>


### [275] [Neural Regression with Embeddings for Numerical Attribute Prediction in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26729)
*Rupesh Sapkota,Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou,Moshood Yekini,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LitEm神经回归模型，使直推式知识图谱嵌入模型能够预测知识图谱中的数值属性；并进一步提出共训练框架联合训练SOTA嵌入模型与LitEm，提升双线性模型的链接预测性能，同时使其具备数值属性预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱通常包含丰富的数值属性，但大多数直推式嵌入模型忽视了这些信息，限制了其对包含多样化信息的真实世界知识图谱的表示能力。

Method: 提出LitEm（神经回归模型）用于预测知识图谱中的数值属性，并设计共训练框架将LitEm与SOTA直推式知识图谱嵌入模型联合训练。

Result: LitEm在FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K和Mutagenesis上对大部分属性取得最佳或次佳结果；共训练框架主要提升双线性模型的链接预测性能，并使其能够预测数值属性；字面量感知评估表明性能提升来自对属性信息的有效编码和利用，而非额外参数。

Conclusion: LitEm及其共训练框架能有效弥补现有直推式知识图谱嵌入模型对数值属性建模的缺失，改善链接预测性能并赋予数值属性预测能力，且代码已公开。

Abstract: In recent years, transductive knowledge graph embedding models have been applied to tasks such as link prediction and query answering. Although knowledge graphs often contain rich numerical attributes, most embedding models neglect them, limiting their ability to represent real-world knowledge graphs with diverse information. In this work, we propose a neural regression model (LitEm) that enables transductive knowledge graph embedding models to predict numerical attributes within knowledge graphs. Experimental results demonstrate that LitEm achieves the best or second-best results on most attributes across FB15K-237, YAGO15K, DB15K, and Mutagenesis. Furthermore, we propose a co-training framework that jointly trains state-of-the-art transductive knowledge graph embedding models with LitEm, which improves link prediction performance mainly for bilinear models and simultaneously enables them to predict numerical attributes. In addition, the literal-awareness evaluation demonstrates that co-training helps models to encode and exploit attribute information in a "literal-aware'' manner, suggesting that the observed gains are not merely due to additional parameters. We publicly release our implementation at https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings.

</details>


### [276] [Rethinking Message Passing as Retrieval for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26732)
*Jintang Li,Yuhong Chen,Ruofan Wu,Binli Luo,Jiayi Ji,Hui Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RTA框架，将GNN的邻域聚合重新概念化为检索增强视角：每层通过MLP处理节点表示和标签感知的检索上下文摘要来做出预测，取代结构化的消息传递。RTA在多个文本属性图基准上匹配甚至超越强GNN和图LLM基线，同时提升效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN（消息传递神经网络）在实践中取得了成功，但其邻域聚合为何优于节点级MLP尚未被清晰解释。此外，消息传递范式计算成本高且对不完美的图结构敏感。作者希望从检索增强的角度重新审视GNN，以提供理论解释并设计更高效、更鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 提出RTA（Retrieval-augmented Text graph Aggregation）框架：一种基于MLP的简单方法，用标签感知的检索和传播（label-aware retrieval and propagation）替代结构化的消息传递。每层通过MLP结合节点表示与置换不变的检索图上下文摘要进行预测。理论上：(i)将基于检索的聚合与softmax注意力消息传递建立联系；(ii)证明检索上下文监督对错误检索异常值的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个文本属性图基准上的实验表明，RTA匹配或超越了强GNN和图LLM基线，同时在不同场景下提高了效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RTA验证了检索增强视角对GNN的有效性：标签感知检索与传播能替代结构消息传递，在保持甚至提升性能的同时显著改善计算效率和结构扰动鲁棒性，为图学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically conceptualized as message-passing neural networks, yet it remains unclear why neighborhood aggregation reliably outperforms node-wise multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Despite its empirical success, this paradigm can be computationally expensive and sensitive to imperfect graph structures. In this work, we present a retrieval-augmented view of GNNs: each layer makes predictions by applying an MLP to a node representation together with a permutation-invariant summary of retrieved graph context. Motivated by this perspective, we propose RTA, a simple MLP-based framework that replaces structural message passing with label-aware retrieval and propagation. We provide theoretical insights that (i) connect retrieval-based aggregation to softmax-attention message passing, and (ii) establish the robustness of retrieved-context supervision to mis-retrieved outliers. Experiments on multiple text-attributed graph benchmarks show that RTA matches or even outperforms strong GNN and graph LLM baselines while improving efficiency and robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [277] [Self-Augmented Diffusion Guidance for Physics-Informed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26748)
*Akira Osaka,Naoya Takeishi,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散引导与自生成数据增强的物理信息扩散模型，通过在采样时显式设置物理偏差条件为零来生成符合物理规律的空时信号（如流体动力学时序图像），无需在每次去噪迭代中求解控制方程，显著降低生成样本的物理偏差，并能与现有物理约束扩散方法结合进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型生成物理现象（如流体动力学时序图像）时无法融入物理定律约束，导致生成的样本视觉上合理但动力学上严重偏离真实物理规律。现有方法需要在去噪过程中反复求解控制方程，计算成本高昂，限制了其在昂贵数值模拟问题上的适用性。

Method: 提出一种物理信息引导的扩散模型方法：1) 学习以物理偏差程度为条件的数据分布；2) 在采样阶段显式将偏差条件设为零以生成符合物理规律的样本；3) 通过自生成数据增强获取带有物理偏差标签的训练数据；4) 将控制方程评估与扩散模型训练和采样解耦，避免在每次去噪迭代中求解控制方程，从而降低计算成本并加速样本生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法相比标准扩散模型显著降低了生成样本的物理偏差；与现有物理约束扩散方法结合时，能够进一步降低偏差，验证了方法的互补性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种简单而有效的物理信息扩散建模框架，在保证生成质量的同时大幅提升物理一致性，且因解耦设计具有良好的计算效率，适用于需要昂贵数值仿真的物理问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models can be used to generate spatiotemporal signals of physical phenomena, such as time-series images of fluid dynamics. However, a major limitation of standard diffusion models is that they do not incorporate constraints derived from the underlying physical laws. Consequently, generated samples may appear visually plausible while deviating substantially from the true dynamics. In this study, we propose a simple yet effective physics-informed approach based on diffusion guidance with self-generated data augmentation. The proposed method learns the data distribution conditioned on the degree of deviation from the physically correct dynamics and generates samples by explicitly setting the deviation condition to be zero. The method decouples the evaluation of the governing equations from the diffusion model training and sampling processes, avoiding the need to solve the governing equations at every iteration of the denoising process. This design makes the method applicable to problems requiring computationally expensive numerical simulations and enables faster sample generation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly reduces the deviations compared with standard diffusion models but also achieves further reductions when combined with existing physics-constrained diffusion methods.

</details>


### [278] [Safety by Design: Realized-Cost Constraints for Contextual Bandits with Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26755)
*Spyros Dragazis,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有一维连续动作的上下文赌博机问题，提出了HPC-UCB算法，在阶段级高概率约束下（对已实现成本而非仅期望成本）进行安全决策。算法采用乐观-悲观策略设计，为线性奖励/成本模型证明了紧致的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，并利用eluder维度将分析推广到一般函数类。实验表明该方法相比期望成本约束基线可显著减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全约束赌博机模型仅约束期望成本低于阈值，但在异方差（heteroscedastic）场景中，动作选择不仅影响期望奖励和成本，还会影响观测结果的方差，仅约束期望成本可能不足以保证安全性。例如剂量选择或自动驾驶等场景中，单次不安全决策就可能导致灾难性后果，因此需要对已实现的成本施加阶段级高概率约束。

Method: 提出High-Probability Constrained UCB (HPC-UCB)算法，采用乐观-悲观（optimistic-pessimistic）策略：在奖励侧采取乐观探索以最大化累积奖励，在成本侧采用保守估计以维持安全动作集合。算法支持一维连续动作空间，分别处理线性奖励/成本模型（证明$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$遗憾界）以及一般函数类（利用eluder维度进行推广分析）。

Result: 对于线性奖励和成本模型，证明了紧致的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$遗憾界；通过eluder维度将该分析扩展到一般函数类。实验表明，相比基于期望成本约束的基线方法，强制执行已实现成本的安全约束能显著降低安全违规次数。

Conclusion: 本文论证了在异方差设置下，传统的期望成本约束不足以保障安全性，必须考虑已实现成本的阶段级高概率约束。HPC-UCB算法在保持最优遗憾界的同时有效降低了安全违规，为安全关键的应用场景提供了更稳健的决策框架。

Abstract: Contextual bandits are a standard framework for sequential decision-making under uncertainty, with applications in clinical trials, dosage selection, recommendation systems, and autonomous systems. Safety is central in many of these applications, since a single unsafe decision in settings such as dosage selection or autonomous driving can have catastrophic consequences. A common way to model safety in bandit problems is to associate each action with both a reward signal and a cost signal, and to optimize reward subject to constraints on cost. Most existing safety-constrained bandit models enforce safety by requiring the expected cost of each action to remain below a prescribed threshold. However, this may be insufficient in heteroscedastic settings, where the chosen action affects not only the expected reward and cost, but also the variability of the observed outcomes. We study contextual bandits with one-dimensional continuous actions and stage-wise high-probability constraints on the realized cost. We propose High-Probability Constrained UCB, an optimistic-pessimistic algorithm that explores for reward while conservatively estimating the safe action set. For linear reward and cost models, we prove a tight $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$ regret bound, and we extend the analysis to general function classes using the eluder dimension. Experiments show that enforcing realized-cost safety substantially reduces violations compared with expected-cost constrained baselines.

</details>


### [279] [On the Indistinguishability of Human v/s AI Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26797)
*Jaee Ponde,Aritra Das,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在拥有人类写作样本的条件下，如何通过策略性改写（paraphrasing）将AI生成文本向人类文本分布逼近。作者在多样本设定下证明，在简单的混合与稳定性条件下，反复改写机器生成的文本会使其分布收敛到经验人类分布，并给出了显式的收敛速率、有限样本分析以及所需人类样本数和改写轮数的规模刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的快速提升，区分AI生成文本与人类写作成为紧迫问题，而改写工具进一步削弱了检测能力。作者希望理解：当攻击者获取到人类写作样本时，如何利用这些样本通过策略性改写使机器文本在分布上更接近人类，从而评估这一威胁的严重性并理解其理论边界。

Method: 作者采用理论分析的方法，在多样本设定（同一提示下同时拥有人类和机器回答）下，将改写过程建模为一种向经验人类分布进行迭代混合与稳定的过程。通过推导在简单混合和稳定性条件下反复改写如何使机器分布收敛到人类经验分布，得到了显式收敛速率，并将分析扩展到有限样本情形，进一步刻画了达到期望误差所需的样本规模与改写轮数。

Result: 证明了重复改写可使得机器文本分布以显式收敛速率向人类经验分布收敛；在有限样本设定下，给出了收敛保证；并量化了所需人类样本数量与改写轮数随目标误差的缩放关系。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上刻画了基于人类样本的对抗性改写攻击的能力边界，揭示了仅需有限的人类样本和改写轮数即可使机器文本在分布层面逼近人类写作，对AI文本检测系统的鲁棒性提出了新的挑战与思考。

Abstract: The rapid improvement of LLMs has made distinguishing AI-generated text from human writing a pressing problem. This challenge is further amplified by paraphrasing tools designed to make machine-generated text appear more "human". We study how access to human writing samples can be used to strategically paraphrase machine-generated responses toward the human distribution. Under a multi-sample setting with human and machine responses to the same prompts, we show that repeated paraphrasing moves the machine distribution toward the empirical human distribution under simple mixing and stability conditions. Our results derive an explicit convergence rate, extend the analysis to a finite-sample setting, and characterize how the required number of human samples and paraphrasing rounds scale with the desired error.

</details>


### [280] [SAGE: Variate-Wise Semantic Augmentation for Vision-Language Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26829)
*Haizhao Fan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，一个基于CLIP的端到端多模态时序预测模型，通过门控残差路径注入变量级语义描述和统计描述符，同时利用冻结的CLIP视觉编码器进行训练期对比学习对齐，在不引入LLM推理的情况下实现最先进的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型仅处理原始数值序列，缺乏领域专家所依赖的语义知识（如变量物理含义、统计行为和时间动态）。已有方法要么在推理时依赖大语言模型导致计算开销高，要么在数据集级别应用统一文本提示而忽略各变量间的异构语义。

Method: 提出SAGE框架：1）CLIP文本编码器处理频率增强的块和变量token；2）门控残差路径注入变量特定描述和统计描述符；3）冻结的CLIP视觉编码器通过仅在训练期间使用的对比目标，将渲染的序列与时间表征对齐；4）双通道使用CLIP提供互补的语义和视觉监督，无需在预测循环中放置LLM。

Result: 在八个长期预测基准和M4数据集上，SAGE取得了最先进的预测精度。消融实验证实了多模态对齐和变量级知识带来的互补性增益。

Conclusion: 通过CLIP的双用途（文本+视觉）设计，SAGE以轻量化的方式将语义和视觉多模态信息融入时序预测，避免了LLM推理的高计算成本，同时超越了现有方法的精度，验证了变量级多模态对齐在时间序列预测中的有效性和互补性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models operate on raw numerical sequences, lacking the semantic knowledge that domain experts implicitly leverage, such as the physical meaning of each variable, its statistical behavior, and its temporal dynamics. Recent efforts to bridge this gap fall into two camps. Some rely on large language models at inference time, which is computationally expensive. Others apply uniform textual prompts at the dataset level, ignoring the heterogeneous semantics across individual variates. We propose SAGE (Seeing and Augmenting with Grounded Encoding), an end-to-end CLIP-based framework that jointly models temporal, cross-variable, textual, and visual information. The CLIP text encoder processes frequency-enhanced patches and variable tokens, while gated residual paths inject variable-specific descriptions and statistical descriptors. In parallel, the frozen CLIP vision encoder aligns rendered series with temporal representations through a training-only contrastive objective. This dual use of CLIP adds complementary semantic and visual supervision without placing an LLM in the forecasting loop. Across eight long-term benchmarks and M4, SAGE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. Ablations confirm complementary gains from multimodal alignment and variable-level knowledge.

</details>


### [281] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Control of CAV Platoon Joining Maneuvers in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26860)
*Biao Yin,Abderrahmane Kasmi,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体建模与SUMO仿真框架，用于研究混合交通环境下CAV车队加入机动策略，对比DQN、DDQN和PPO三种深度强化学习算法，发现PPO在加入成功率和碰撞率方面表现最优，但存在安全与决策效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV编队行驶能提升道路安全和通行能力，但在真实交通中面临不确定性和异质驾驶行为的挑战。强化学习虽具有解决此类控制问题的潜力，但其实际部署涉及安全性和学习效率问题，尤其是在CAV与人类驾驶车辆共存的混合交通环境中，需要系统性地研究和验证不同DRL算法的表现。

Method: 构建一个通用的建模和仿真框架，将智能体建模与SUMO交通仿真器耦合。在奖励函数中融入风险行为惩罚，或采用外部安全控制器约束学习策略。在该框架下对比评估DQN、DDQN和PPO三种深度强化学习算法，用于CAV车队加入机动控制。

Result: PPO算法表现优于DQN和DDQN，加入成功率约98%，碰撞率低于1%，主要得益于奖励函数中的风险相关惩罚。但PPO需要更多决策步骤完成机动，揭示了安全、加入效果和决策效率之间的权衡。外部安全控制器能有效防止碰撞，但可能降低加入效率。

Conclusion: 研究强调在混合交通中设计基于强化学习的CAV车队加入控制器时，必须同时考虑安全性和效率因素。PPO在安全与效率的平衡上表现最佳，但不同安全机制各有优劣，需要根据实际需求选择合适的控制策略。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicle (CAV) platooning offers a promising approach to improving road safety and traffic capacity. However, platoon control in real-world traffic is challenging due to uncertainty and heterogeneous driving behaviors. Reinforcement learning (RL) has strong potential for addressing such control problems, but its practical deployment raises challenges related to safety and learning efficiency. This paper proposes a generic modeling and simulation framework for investigating CAV platoon joining maneuvers and comparing deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based control algorithms. The problem is particularly challenging in mixed-traffic environments, where CAVs coexist with human-driven vehicles exhibiting heterogeneous longitudinal and lateral behaviors. The objective is to achieve safe and efficient joining maneuvers by either incorporating penalties for risky behaviors into the learning process or using an external safety controller to constrain the learned policy. An agent-based modeling framework coupled with the Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) simulator is used to evaluate Deep Q-Network (DQN), Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN), and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Results show that PPO outperforms DQN and DDQN, achieving a joining success rate of approximately 98 % and a collision rate below 1 %, largely due to risk-related penalties incorporated into the reward function. However, this improved performance requires more decision steps to complete the maneuver, revealing a trade-off between safety, joining effectiveness, and decision efficiency. An external safety controller effectively prevents collisions, although its interventions may reduce joining efficiency. The results highlight the importance of jointly considering safety and efficiency when designing RL-based controllers for CAV platoon joining in mixed traffic.

</details>


### [282] [Mitigating Strong-Modality Collapse in Multimodal Learning via Inverted Asymmetric Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26879)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Inverted Asymmetric Fusion (IAF)框架解决多模态融合中"强模态坍缩"问题——即主导模态在融合后被削弱导致模型无法超越单模态基线。IAF通过让主导模态原样通过融合层、弱模态以其为上下文锚点，并结合模态感知知识蒸馏增强弱模态，在三个数据集上超越最强单模态基线最高达8.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合预期能提升模型性能，但在MultiHuSE数据集上，早期、晚期和对称注意力融合经常无法超越最佳单模态基线（文本）。通过路径隔离分析发现，融合后文本路径的准确率从74.9%降至56.4%，说明主导模态在融合过程中被劣化。作者将这种现象称为"强模态坍缩"（strong-modality collapse），并认为这是多模态模型无法超越单模态基线的重要原因。

Method: 提出Inverted Asymmetric Fusion (IAF)框架：(1) 避免在模态间强制施加相互注意力，主导模态通过融合层时保持不变（pass-through）；(2) 弱模态以主导模态为上下文锚点进行注意力交互；(3) 在融合前使用Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation增强弱模态。

Result: 在三个具有不同模态层级的数据集上评估：文本主导的MultiHuSE和UR-FUNNY，以及音视频主导的MUStARD。路径隔离显示IAF在所有测试配置中将主导模态的内部准确率保持在单模态上限，而对称融合在MultiHuSE上使主导模态退化最高达18.5%。IAF相比最强单模态基线提升了最多8.25%。

Conclusion: IAF通过保留主导模态信息和增强弱模态来缓解强模态坍缩，有效克服了对称融合架构的固有缺陷，使多模态模型能够真正超越单模态基线。

Abstract: Fusing multiple modalities is expected to improve model performance. However, on the MultiHuSE dataset, early, late, and symmetric attention fusion often fail to outperform the best unimodal baseline (text). Pathway isolation of a symmetric attention fusion model reveals that the text-pathway accuracy drops from 74.9% to 56.4% after fusion in one such setting, indicating that the dominant modality can be degraded during integration. We term this strong-modality collapse and argue that it helps explain why some multimodal models fail to surpass unimodal baselines. We propose Inverted Asymmetric Fusion (IAF), which avoids forcing mutual attention across modalities. The dominant modality is preserved by passing through fusion unchanged, while weaker modalities attend to it as a contextual anchor. Before fusion, weaker modalities are strengthened using Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation. We evaluate IAF on three benchmarks with different modality hierarchies: text-dominant datasets (MultiHuSE, UR-FUNNY) and an audio-visual-dominant dataset (MUStARD). Pathway isolation shows that IAF preserves the dominant modality's internal accuracy at its unimodal ceiling across all tested configurations, whereas symmetric fusion degrades it by up to 18.5% on MultiHuSE. IAF improves over the strongest unimodal baseline by up to 8.25%.

</details>


### [283] [A Layer Importance Metric for Quantization Accounting for the Speed-Quality Trade-off in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26926)
*Artem Safronov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种复合度量指标，用于在小语言模型（sLLM）上优先选择量化加速目标。该指标结合了信息保留（基于归一化SQNR系数）和吞吐量增益（基于roofline模型的延迟分析）两个正交准则，通过Gemma 3 1B模型验证表明FFN块和嵌入矩阵是最有潜力的加速目标，无需实际执行即可估计加速速度（预测误差约4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型部署在内存和计算资源受限的设备上，其自回归推理受内存带宽限制。由于sLLM架构冗余度有限且只有少数层对低精度不敏感，均匀量化往往对这些模型有害。因此需要一种能准确识别哪些层适合量化加速的方法。

Method: 提出一种复合度量，由两个正交标准组成：(1) 信息保留能力，通过归一化的SQNR系数衡量；(2) 吞吐量增益，通过基于roofline模型的延迟分析建模。方法包括：在Gemma 3 1B上分析定位FFN块和嵌入矩阵为最佳加速目标；对每个候选块估计基于模拟量化的归一化质量分数和基于roofline建模的归一化速度分数（无需实际执行）；将两个分数合成为复合优先级系数以调节速度与质量的权衡。

Result: 在多种模型架构上评估表明，加速速度的预测误差约为4%。相比进化搜索、专用加速器或基于Shapley值的方法，该方法能将更多资源分配给最具表现力的层。

Conclusion: 该分析方法是通用的，可用于优先选择单独的块、投影子层或整个Transformer层作为量化目标，使sLLM量化成为一个可预测的工程任务。该方法对层的选择优于进化搜索、专用加速器和基于Shapley值的方法。

Abstract: Small language models (sLLMs) are nowadays hosted on devices with limited memory and computational budget. In an autoregressive setup, inference is memory-bandwidth bound: uniform quantization is often detrimental to such models, since their architecture has limited redundancies and only a few layers are not very sensitive to lower precision. We propose a composite metric that combines two orthogonal criteria: information retention (measured in terms of a normalized SQNR-based coefficient) and throughput gains (modeled using a roofline-based latency analysis). By profiling Gemma 3 1B, we find that Feed-Forward Network blocks and the embedding matrix are the most promising targets for acceleration. For each candidate, we estimate a normalized quality score based on simulated quantization and a normalized speed score based on roofline modeling with no actual execution needed. We combine the two scores in a composite priority coefficient, allowing us to tune the trade-off between speed and quality as needed. Our metric is general and can be used to prioritize individual blocks, their projection sublayers, or transformer layers as a whole. We evaluate our approach on several model architectures, showing that our estimates have at around 4% prediction error for the accelerated speedup. We find that our method generally allocates more resources to the most expressive layers compared to evolutionary search, specialized accelerators, or Shapley-value-based approaches that require expensive approximate inference. Our analytical approach makes sLLM quantization a predictable engineering task.

</details>


### [284] [Scaling Model-Generated Distillation Data Can Make Latent Teacher Traits More Recoverable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26958)
*Zhichen Dong,Zhixuan Liu,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Shuyang Zhang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，扩大模型生成数据的规模不仅改善蒸馏效果，还会放大教师模型中微妙的、与特定特质相关的信号，即使训练数据是离题的且不提及该特质。更大规模的数据会让教师在学生模型中留下的特质印记更加清晰，这一效应跨模型族、特质类型、多特质设置和跨模型迁移均成立，提示生成数据的蒸馏需要特质感知的策展与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为扩大模型生成数据（蒸馏数据）的规模总是有益的——更多样例能提升覆盖率、降低噪声、产生更强的学生模型。本文旨在揭示一个此前被忽视的第二效应：更大规模的数据会放大教师模型中细微的、特质相关的信号在学生模型中的可检测性，即使这些数据是离题且不涉及该特质的。

Method: 采用受潜意识学习启发的受控实验设计。教师模型被诱导表达某种目标特质，生成受限的离题数据（如仅数字的补全）。学生在不同规模的独立离题数据上训练，并在独立领域评估，使用匹配的无特质对照组来隔离特质特异性迁移。同时分析学生模型中的LoRA更新以验证行为层面的发现。

Result: 主要发现是更大的独立数据集会使教师的诱导特质在学生后续行为中更突出。其他可能的特质也可能随规模增强，但目标特质通常增长更多。小规模学生原本偏好目标特质时，扩展主要放大该行为；若偏好相关或显著替代特质，更多数据可将行为转向目标特质。LoRA更新的分析呈现平行趋势。这些效应在多个模型族、特质类型、多特质设置和跨模型迁移中均成立。

Conclusion: 扩大生成蒸馏数据的规模应配合特质感知的策展与评估，即便数据看似离题或良性。这意味着数据规模不仅有量变效果，还可能改变学生模型习得的隐性特质分布，需要在实践中加以监测和管理。

Abstract: Scaling model-generated data is usually viewed as improving distillation: more examples should increase coverage, reduce noise, and produce stronger students. We show a second effect: larger datasets can make subtle teacher-specific signals easier to detect in the trained student, even when examples are off-task and never mention the trait. In a controlled setup inspired by subliminal learning, a teacher induced to express a target trait generates restricted off-task data, such as number-only completions. Students trained on different amounts of independent off-task data are evaluated in a separate domain, with matched no-trait controls isolating target-specific transfer. Our main finding is that larger independent datasets make the teacher's induced trait stand out more clearly in the student's later behavior. Other plausible traits may also strengthen with scale, but the target usually grows more. When the small-scale student already favors the target, scaling mainly amplifies that behavior; when it favors a related or salient alternative, more data can shift behavior toward the intended trait. Analyses of learned LoRA updates show a parallel trend. These effects appear across model families, trait types, multi-trait settings, and cross-model transfer. Our results suggest that scaling generated distillation data should be paired with trait-aware curation and evaluation, even when the data appears off-task or benign.

</details>


### [285] [Packora: Systematic Design for Generative Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26962)
*Nayoung Kim,Kiyoung Seong,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Packora，一种基于流的分子晶体结构预测(CSP)生成模型，能从分子图联合预测原子坐标和晶格参数，支持多组分和有机金属晶体，并可条件于构象子集、立体化学标签和空间群信息。在生成和排序基准上均优于基线，实现最佳匹配预算覆盖率、更高的实验形态恢复率和更低的实验形态排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体结构预测在制药、农化和有机电子等领域至关重要，因为分子构象与堆积的细微差异会显著影响材料性质。现有方法在生成质量和端到端性能上仍有不足，需要一个能同时支持多组分、有机金属体系且可灵活条件化的统一生成模型。

Method: Packora是一个基于流的生成模型，从分子图联合预测原子坐标和晶格。它采用可缓存的成对推理架构，设计了针对性的训练目标与数值求解器，引入条件化dropout技术，并平衡扩展成对与单表示。模型支持对任意构象子集、立体化学标签和空间群信息进行条件化，并采用生成与排序分离的评估方案。

Result: Packora在所有六个生成基准上取得最佳匹配预算覆盖率，在排序基准上实现更高的实验形态恢复率、更低的实验形态排名以及更快的收敛速度，全面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Packora通过系统的架构、训练、条件化、推理和扩展研究，确立了一种有效的CSP生成模型设计范式，为分子晶体结构预测提供了更优的端到端解决方案，并验证了生成与排序分离评估框架的有效性。

Abstract: Molecular crystal structure prediction (CSP) is important in pharmaceuticals, agrochemicals, and organic electronics, where subtle differences in molecular conformation and packing can strongly affect material properties. We present Packora, a flow-based generative model for molecular CSP that jointly predicts atomic coordinates and the lattice from molecular graphs. Packora supports multi-component and organometallic crystals and can condition on any subset of molecular conformers, stereochemical labels, and space-group information within a single model. Inspired by the CCDC CSP blind test, we evaluate generation and ranking separately, using generation to isolate generator quality and ranking to measure end-to-end performance under a common relaxation and ranking pipeline. We also systematically study architecture, training, conditioning, inference, and scaling, identifying an effective design based on cacheable pairwise reasoning, training objective and numerical solver choices, conditioning dropout, and balanced scaling of pairwise and single representations.
